# Do Habits: Get It Done (formerly "Done") — App Store ID 1103961876 — Full Review Analysis

**App:** Do Habits: Get It Done — *Daily Routine & Goal Planner* (listed for most of its life as **"Done: A Simple Habit Tracker"**)
**Developer of record in the corpus metadata:** Kodeon, Inc. · **Bundle:** `com.tbd.Done` · **Developers named by reviewers:** Treebetty (2016–2020), Reflectly ApS (from Dec 2020)
**Corpus:** 5,186 written reviews · 80 storefronts · 20 May 2016 → 7 September 2026
**Corpus mean rating:** 2.355 · **Extracted:** 8 September 2026 (`itunes MZStore userReviewsRow`)
**Analysis date:** 11 September 2026
**Business model as reviewers experience it:** free download with a hard **3-habit** free tier. 2016–Aug 2020: a one-time "Pro/Premium" in-app purchase ($3.99 → ~$9.99). 31 Aug 2020 onward: annual subscription, later bundled with sibling apps ("Growth Bundle"). From Aug 2023: a re-introduced "lifetime" purchase sold through perpetual countdown offers. Upsell pop-ups; no third-party ads.

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified finding gives **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal label, and review IDs**. Any claim traces back to the exact record via `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`. **Part 11.6 lists every review ID behind every theme, in full.**
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive** — one review can carry several themes.
- Signal thresholds used throughout: `<0.1%` ignore · `0.1–0.5%` weak · `0.5–1%` emerging · `1–3%` meaningful · `3–5%` very strong · `>5%` high-priority.
- **Facts** are what reviewers wrote. **Interpretation** is labelled as such.
- Global denominator is **5,186** unless stated. Era denominators: E1 941 · E2 570 · E3 1,866 · E4 1,636 · E5 173.

### ⚠️ Seven warnings before any number here is used

1. **One 25-day event dominates the corpus.** Between 22 Dec 2021 and 15 Jan 2022, 1,135 reviews arrived (**21.89% of all reviews**), mean 1.44, 79.4% 1★. 918 of them say the app would not open. That window alone holds **35.0% of every 1★ review ever written** for this app (901 of 2,571). Excluding it, the corpus mean is **2.61** (not 2.36) and the 1★ share is **41.2%** (not 49.6%). Where the wave distorts a comparison, both numbers are given.
2. **This listing is four different products over ten years.** A one-time-purchase indie app (2016–Aug 2020); the same app on subscription (Aug 2020); a new owner (Dec 2020); a v2 rebuild (Mar 2022); a v3 redesign (Jan 2023); a rename to "Do" (~Apr 2025). Era comparisons compare different products, and reviews of the 2018 app are not evidence about the 2025 app.
3. **No `version` field exists.** Release attribution comes only from what reviewers write (they name 1.3.1, 1.6, 1.19.0, 1.19.1, 2.0.2, 2.0.8, 2.1.2, 2.7.1, 2.7.2, 3.0, 3.0.1). It is never asserted from metadata.
4. **"Payer" evidence is self-selected.** 625 reviewers (12.05%) say in the first person that they paid. Almost half of them (304) report that what they paid for was not delivered or not honoured. That is a sample of payers *with a problem*, not a sample of customers.
5. **Short-review inflation is *not* a factor here.** Unlike some peers, reviews with a body of 25 characters or less never exceed 7.7% of any era, and review-prompt complaints are 20 (0.39%). The low rating is not an artefact of prompting; it is what people wrote at length (median body 160 characters).
6. **Theme rates are not equally comparable across languages.** The classifier covers English, Spanish, Portuguese, French, German and partial Italian, Dutch, Scandinavian, Polish, Czech, Turkish, Russian, Chinese, Japanese, Korean and Vietnamese patterns. Coverage is thinnest outside English; where coverage is thin, a theme is *under*-counted. Country comparisons therefore lead with **rating distributions**, which are language-neutral.
7. **Price claims are not reconcilable.** Reviewers quote 60+ price points across 15 currencies, plus promotional, first-year, renewal, bundle and "lifetime" prices, and anchor prices as high as CA$499.99. Part 2.3 reports the direction of travel, not a price list.

---

# EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

**1. Purchase integrity is the business-critical failure, and it is the dominant story of 2023–2026.**
**468 reviews (9.02%, high-priority), mean 1.23, 399 of them 1★** say Premium they paid for was not granted, was taken away, or could not be restored. That is **24.5% of all 2023–24 reviews (400 of 1,636)**. The complaint comes from two distinct groups:
- **189 legacy buyers** (3.64%): a one-time or "lifetime" purchase from 2016–2021 was later downgraded to Basic, or "Restore purchase" failed. IDs: `9833924285`, `9835947274`, `9839668950`, `9863669849`, `10430541861`, `10489995181`, `11505353898`, `13465804405`, `14196021819`.
- **295 buyers of the 2023+ "$19.99 lifetime" offer** (5.69%): they were charged, but the app stayed on Basic and kept showing the same offer. IDs: `10319481379`, `10324957727`, `10333454546`, `10391681656`, `10413198433`, `10639028732`, `10695671832`, `11393421407`, `11689173519`, `11727458734`.

Widen to everything that touches money-handling — plus 69 discount offers that checked out at full price, 66 cancellation traps and 403 reviews using billing/refund/scam language — and it is **768 reviews (14.81%), mean 1.30**. That is **52.4% of all 1★ reviews written in 2023–24 (484 of 924)**.

**2. A support vacuum turns every defect into a 1★.** **460 reviews (8.87%, high-priority), mean 1.32** say support never replied or that the contact path itself was broken. That is **20.3% of 2023–24 reviews and 20.8% of 2025–26 reviews**, against 0.6% in 2016–2020. Against that stand only **18 reviews that praise support, 13 of them before September 2020**. Evidence: `6738427534` (Dec 2020, told the app had been acquired and given a new address that never answered), `8263579754`, `9668372318`, `10430541861` (five emails), `10623891834` ("two years"), `11101822527`, `12473833371` (a refund promised and not issued), `14196021819` (contacted the developer as its reply asked; no answer). Broken contact paths: `7324774905`, `7463934457`, `8381379162`, `9601921752`, `13465804405` (support address bounced).

**3. One release defect produced a third of the app's lifetime 1★ reviews.**
**1,052 reviews (20.29%, high-priority), mean 1.30** say the app would not open, crashed on launch, or was stuck on its first screen. **918 of them fall in 25 days (22 Dec 2021 → 15 Jan 2022)**, peaking at 208 reviews on 3 January 2022. The wave coincides with:
- **version 1.19.0**, named in `8193092532`, `8200043639`, `8184394543` ("update of 22.12.2021") and `8204886004` (1.19.1);
- a **TikTok-driven install surge**: TikTok is named in 57 wave reviews and 98 reviews overall, 81 of them in 2022.

51% of wave reviews come from outside the US, and 47 wave reviewers say they never got to use the app at all. It was resolved by an update around 7–9 January (`8217777536`, `8223042075`, `8229706176`). Some long-time users lost years of data along the way (`8189189742`, `8218402912`, `8263579754`). Smaller repeats followed in October 2021 (intro screen froze) and May–June 2022 (hang after an update).

**4. The 3-habit cap has always been there; what changed in 2020 is how reviewers took it.**
**670 reviews (12.92%, high-priority)** complain about the free cap, and it is present in every era: 13.5% of 2016–20 reviews, 18.4% of 2020–21 and 14.0% of 2023–24. Monetisation friction as a whole is **1,659 reviews (31.99%), mean 1.90**. The same cap drew very different reactions in the one-time-purchase era:
- Price praise was **6.7%** of E1 reviews; it has run at **0.7–1.7%** ever since.
- **27 reviews (mean 4.78, all in E1)** single out "one time, no subscription" as the reason they paid. `3441366071` argues that a subscription "is 'hooked on a drug' then being repeatedly charged"; also `2322753612`, `2405289516`, `5368023814`.
- Price objection jumped from **4.1% (E1) to 13.0% (E2)** the moment the one-time option was removed (`6380661176`, `6395873344`, `6422603877`).

**5. The upsell became a UX defect in its own right.**
- **676 reviews (13.04%)** complain about upsell pop-ups and banners. Share by era: **1.9% (E1) → 14.9% (E2) → 22.6% (E4)**.
- **314 (6.05%)** of those complaints come from people who had *already paid*.
- **111 (2.14%)** describe a "limited-time" countdown that resets and never ends (`6837024394`, `7710455837`, `10817420229` "Groundhog Day", `11443853483` "resetting for the past 4 months").
- **19** describe it appearing **twice per launch** from January 2024.

Reviewers say the permanent banner slowed the app (`9819638240` "takes 30 seconds to mark off", `9839487554`, `10001218229`) and, in December 2020, hid their bottom habit (`6751801484`, `6753490588`). The sharpest statement comes from `9942976990`: "Given that the average workflow is to open the app, quickly mark off a completed habit, and close the app this popup is completely unacceptable".

**6. Redesigns removed what long-time users relied on.** Feature-removal complaints total **192 (3.70%)**, **9.35% of 2023–24 reviews**:
- **monthly/yearly goal frequency, 58 reviews** (removed Dec 2022 – Jan 2023);
- **streak count taken off the main list, 52** (v3.0, 27 Jan 2023; partially restored ~20 Feb);
- **swipe between days, 41**;
- also quit-to-zero goals, Shortcuts support, the tappable widget, custom ordering, the "last done" time, and editing past entries.

"An update made it worse" appears in **215 reviews (4.15%)**. Representative: `9438711434`, `9526250306`, `9560996028` (tracked a 30-day pill cycle by the streak), `9561693468`, `9564490880`, `9591160875`, `8055151737` (relied on the old widget to manage a memory disability).

**7. Performance and data trust eroded after 2022.**
- **Freeze/lag, 404 reviews (7.79%)**, reaching **26.6% of 2025–26 reviews**. Reviewers describe taps taking 5–45 seconds to register: `8461640396`, `12066404983` ("can't cope with too many entries"), `12068066761`, `12299152685`, `13808211870`.
- **Miscounted data, 107 reviews.** Includes v2.7.1/2.7.2 inflating counts to "58,720 times this week" (`9131616072`, `9126313490`, `9128448532`) and Apple Health doubling steps or sleep (37 reviews).
- **Lost data, 94 reviews** (`7377929999`, `8583843842` "40 days clean … now 16", `10775645224`).
- Time-zone changes silently rewrite long streaks (`9998476908` "600+ day streak", `11133834669` "1500 days").

**8. The core product is loved, consistently, in every era.**
- Simplicity and ease: **458 reviews rated 4–5★ (8.83%)**.
- Design, colours and sounds: **250 rated 4–5★ (4.82%)**.
- Motivation, streaks and accountability: **200 (3.86%)**.
- A concrete outcome claimed: **113 (2.18%)**, e.g. `7462019995` (34 kg lost), `6785378270` (95-day medication streak), `5968630345` (gums stopped bleeding), `6100935111` (Ménière's disease tracking).

The differentiators reviewers name are specific: building *and* quitting habits in one model; "N times per week" goals without fixed days; the musical completion sounds; and unit tracking. The 2016–2020 app averaged **3.94 (53.6% 5★)**, and the best 2024–26 reviews still praise exactly those things (`11121493895` "tried out 40+ habit tracker apps", `12220855265`, `13701503325`, `13790904255`).

**9. The unmet needs are stable, specific and mostly old:**
- iPad, Mac, web or landscape support (98);
- sync and backup failures (83);
- scheduling gaps — every N days, skip or vacation, specific-day visibility, day start for shift workers (84);
- localisation (61);
- a working Apple Watch app (60);
- monthly frequency (58);
- social or shared lists (49);
- one-off tasks (28);
- VoiceOver and larger touch targets (19).

**10. Cheapest wins, in evidence order:**
1. Honour every past purchase and fix the purchase → entitlement → "gift" flow.
2. Answer support.
3. Never show an upsell to a paying user; drop the fake countdown and the double pop-up.
4. Make the displayed price the charged price, and put cancellation one tap away.
5. Fix tap latency and data integrity (time zones, week start, Health de-duplication).
6. Restore monthly goals, the list streak and swipe-between-days.
7. Only then, test the free cap.

---

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role |
|---|---|
| `reviews.jsonl` | Primary corpus — 5,186 records, one JSON object per line |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 80 per-storefront files |
| `manifest.json` | App metadata, per-storefront counts, rating distribution |
| `_state.json` | Extraction state (not used for analysis) |

## 1.2 Schema

Every record carries: `review_id`, `app_id`, `app_name`, `country`, `country_name`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `author`, `date` (ISO 8601 UTC), `vote_count`, `vote_sum`, `is_edited`.

Fields used: `review_id`, `country`, `rating`, `title`, `body`, `date`, `is_edited`, `vote_count`.
Fields deliberately ignored: `author` (personal data, no analytical value), `app_id`, `app_name` (constant).
`vote_count` is non-zero on 716 records but heavily concentrated: the top-voted review is `8202074659` (120 votes, 4★, a wave review about not being able to open the app), followed by `2463611317` (86 votes, 5★, 2018). Votes are too sparse to weight, so they are only noted.
**No `version` field exists.**

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Records in `reviews.jsonl` | **5,186** |
| Unique `review_id` values | **5,186** (zero ID duplicates) |
| Sum of all 80 `by_country/*.jsonl` line counts | **5,186** — exact match; the ID sets are identical |
| `manifest.json` → `total_reviews` | 5,186 — exact match |
| `manifest.json` → `reviews_per_country` (80 entries) | matches computed per-country counts **exactly, all 80** |
| `manifest.json` → `rating_distribution` | `{5:976, 4:452, 3:581, 2:606, 1:2571}` — matches **exactly** |
| `manifest.json` → `mean_rating` 2.355 | computed 2.3552 — match |
| Empty `body` / empty `title` | **0 / 0** |
| Exact `title`+`body` duplicate text | **4 groups, 11 records**, all with distinct IDs, countries and dates: <br>• `("good","good")` = `2293090314`, `2322838996`, `8500548185`, `11674656531` <br>• `("amazing","amazing")` = `9463782454`, `9781857290`, `10719345043` <br>• `("won't open","app won't open")` = `8174910703`, `8196336945` <br>• `("bad","i can't open the app")` = `8197843384`, `8198859300` <br>**No records were removed.** The denominator throughout is 5,186. |
| `is_edited = true` | 20 records, treated as normal: `4012862730`, `6896878301`, `8491993481`, `8500276223`, `8503202456`, `8866748671`, `9797672949`, `10381964851`, `11815006494`, `11834026398`, `11971429618`, `12031381895`, `12280573976`, `12299152685`, `12345680113`, `12473833371`, `13026590717`, `13602037395`, `13660661446`, `14196021819`. Several are dated update logs, e.g. `13602037395` (Sep 2024 → Jan 2026), `12554960249` (Mar 2024 → Apr 2025), `8756283245` (crash → fixed). |
| Date range | 2016-05-20 → 2026-09-07 (10.3 years) |

**Every one of the 5,186 records was read in full**, in date order, in 26 batches of about 200 reviews. Nothing was sampled or keyword-selected. One-word reviews, emoji-only bodies, non-English reviews and the entire 1,135-review crash wave were all read and all counted. Bodies longer than 1,500 characters were wrapped rather than truncated, so that nothing was cut off.

## 1.4 Processing method

1. **Parse and clean.** Parsed all 5,186 records programmatically, double HTML-unescaped titles and bodies, and collapsed whitespace.
2. **Read everything first.** Emitted the corpus as 26 human-readable batches (`id|country|rating|date|title :: body`) and read all 26 in full. Reading notes were kept with review IDs, and the theme taxonomy was built inductively from those notes before any pattern was written.
3. **Classify.** Built a multilingual regex classifier for 82 themes (including the flags derived in the correction pass) over lowercased `title + body` (definitions in Part 11.2), plus 4 union themes.
4. **Validate precision, then recall.**
   - *Precision:* read every match of each high-impact theme (entitlement failure, discount charged at full price, streak removal, ADHD, monthly frequency) and targeted false-positive scans of the others.
   - *Recall:* for 8 themes, a deliberately broad pattern pulled every *untagged* review containing a related word, and those candidates were read. Part 11.3 lists every ID added or removed.
5. **Corrections made before any number here was used:**
   - `adhd` initially matched the verb "add" ("I went to add, …"). The bare-"add" rule was dropped: **33** (from 36).
   - `monthly_yearly_request` initially matched pricing text ("$60 a year … option"). It was replaced by a **hand-read list of 58** (from a noisy 96).
   - `entitlement_failure` initially swept in "I paid and still see ads" (a different problem) and crash reviews that mentioned paying. **97 false positives were removed; 136 real cases from the recall read were added: 468.**
   - `promo_charged_full` initially matched "instead of 0", "3 goals instead of 5", etc. **33 false positives were removed and 16 recall cases added: 69.** A stricter sub-flag, "actually charged", covers 49 of them.
   - `launch_fail` missed wave phrasings ("took me out immediately", "won't let me on"). Extra patterns plus a wave-window rule added **151**; 8 false positives were removed: **1,052**.
   - `streak_removed`: **24 recall additions → 52**. `cancel_difficulty`: **22 additions → 66**. `support_unresponsive`: **+13 → 460**. `support_positive` is a hand-read list (**18**).
   - `payer` dropped conditional phrasings ("unless I got premium", "thinking if I pay"): **625**.
6. **Split and aggregate.** Split ambiguous mention themes (price, Health, Watch, widget, sync, sounds) into a *mention* theme and a *problem* theme. Aggregated by rating, country, year, month and era, and computed key aggregates both with and without the 22 Dec 2021 – 15 Jan 2022 wave.

## 1.5 Known limitations

- **The wave is the dominant methodological fact.** Every global rate includes it. Rates for reliability themes are therefore inflated globally, and rates for everything else are *diluted* in 2022. Era tables (Part 8) are the corrective.
- **Regex classification has error in both directions.** Manual reading of all 5,186 records is the control, and the corrections in 1.4 are the disclosed outcome. Residual error is highest for Japanese, Korean, Chinese, Thai, Vietnamese, Arabic, Turkish and Indonesian text, where themes are under-counted.
- **Rating is not a feature preference.** 35 cap complaints are 5★ and 47 are 4★ — people who like the app and object to the gate. Conversely, 104 reviews that praise simplicity are 1★ ("simple app shouldn't cost $60/yr"). Positive themes are therefore also reported as the **"rated 4–5★" subset**.
- **Storefront ≠ nationality ≠ language.** Many non-US storefront reviews are in English, and several US reviews are in Spanish. Country findings are **storefront** findings.
- **Unverifiable individual claims are flagged, not counted as fact.** Examples: one reviewer claims €1,600 of bi-weekly charges over three years (`11443832766`); another claims charges were taken "not through Apple" (`10323742685`). Both are recorded as reviewer statements.
- **No download, revenue, retention, churn or refund-outcome data exists in this corpus.** Review volume is used only as a disclosed proxy and is never called downloads.
- **No external source was consulted.** No website, App Store listing, press material or market data was used. Every product, feature and pricing claim in Parts 2–3 is attested by at least one review ID. The one external *assumption* is the membership of the "high-spend markets" group in Part 7.3, flagged there.
- **4,707 of the 5,186 records (90.8%) carry at least one theme (the `payer` flag alone does not count). The remaining 479 (9.2%) are mostly short affective reviews** such as "Good", "Love it" or "Great app". Their mean rating is 3.1 and their median body is 75 characters. They are counted in every denominator.

The daily peak is **2022-01-03: 208 reviews, 173 of them launch failures.**

**Storefronts:** 80. Nine clear the 50-review bar — us 2,657, gb 510, ca 404, au 193, de 155, br 91, nl 85, mx 57, fr 53 — for **4,205 reviews, 81.08%**. The remaining 71 storefronts hold 981 reviews (18.92%), and those are included in every global number.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETISATION MODEL

## 2.1 Feature inventory derived from reviews

Everything here is attested by review text. Nothing is taken from the store listing.

| Capability | Attested by | Free / Paid as reviewers describe it |
|---|---|---|
| Habits to **build** *and* habits to **quit / reduce** in one list | `1515456440`, `3304229802`, `6624535423` ("the ONLY app I've found that does that") | Free up to the cap |
| **Frequency per day / week / month / year** with "N times per period" (no fixed days needed) | `4191961911`, `4879659323`, `5368023814`, `6613616649` | Monthly & yearly **removed Dec 2022 – Jan 2023** (Part 4.5) |
| Specific weekdays; "not tracked today" state | `1429098825` (requested 2016), `9387705098`, `8322360433` | — |
| Unit tracking: count, minutes, custom units, custom increments | `2313790392`, `3752603970`, `5128094125`, `6053003661` | — |
| Musical completion sounds and haptics | `2564271918`, `3675112801`, `5255921582`, `5546153028` | Free |
| Streaks: current and longest | `4348403182`, `3264082010`, `10203803324` | Removed from the main list in v3.0 (Part 4.5) |
| Notes / journal per entry; timestamps | `1531163399` (added Jan 2017), `3877589027`, `3904790978`, `2463611317` | — |
| Charts: week / month / year; per-habit calendar | `2283332022`, `4815348275`, `5527429884` | Described as weak by power users (`4173454628`) |
| Groups / categories; filters | `2301362573` (groups added Mar 2018), `2348669818` | Paid (`2283332022`) |
| Multiple reminders per habit | `3014141825`, `5486707078`, `6624535423` | Paid in E1 (`3606137601`, `3014141825`) |
| Widget: tappable Today-view widget (2018–2021); non-interactive home / lock-screen widget (from Oct 2021) | `4586063685`, `3628645292`, `7862683254`, `9050514368` | Old widget free; new widget limited to 4 habits (Part 4.5) |
| Apple Watch app + complication | `3626589635`, `4586063685`, `5348354820` | Broken for long periods (Part 4.4) |
| Apple Health integration (limited set of habits: steps, sleep, exercise minutes) | `5762715894` ("only a small selection"), `6613933545` ("10 or so preset tasks"), `9252022515` | Counts often wrong (Part 4.4) |
| Backup to Dropbox / iCloud; CSV export | `1611868569`, `3332657537`, `4392697150`, `5325844208` | Manual only; repeated backup nag (`4334400703`, `6288116547`) |
| Passcode / Touch ID lock | `1467315756` (requested), `3873301504` (moved behind the paywall, 2019), `8235097987` | Paid from 2019 |
| Day-start offset for late sleepers / shift workers | `5443554210`, `6359341740`, `7393730415` (capped at 5 am) | Later removed (`8770030841`) |
| Siri Shortcuts | `7109975257`, `3205148873` (requested 2018) | Removed in the 2022 rebuild (`8463235133`) |

| Sibling apps bundle ("Growth Bundle": Tally, Do, Grateful, Moody, Last, Time Block, and later Reflectly) | `3581875318` (first mention, Dec 2018), `5948549297`, `7901290433`, `11061845555` | Paid bundle |

**Not present, per reviewers:**
- a native iPad layout or landscape mode (`4196535080`, `6150612151`, `6731273030`);
- Mac, web or Android versions (`5072257933`, `10854344897`, `8888523405`);
- real-time sync across devices (`5395293570`, `7525700298`, `12283528605`);
- a vacation or skip mode (`5290830053`, `13912782700`);

- social or shared lists (`7487879340`, `10231268067`);
- a monthly billing plan (`12652123252`, `12661738139`);
- Family Sharing for the in-app purchase (`4083584688`, `7103673004`);
- VoiceOver support (`6988004094`, `8200488157`, `9043645768`);
- a UI in any language but English (Part 3.5).

## 2.2 Timeline of the business, as reviewers lived it

| When (from reviews) | Event | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| May 2016 | Launch as "Done" by Treebetty. Free tier = **2 items**; one-time Pro £2.99 / ~$4 | `1396394692`, `1415782159`, `1455609889`, `1489475202` |
| Nov–Dec 2016 | Launch-season freeze on adding a first habit; the developer ships a fix after an email | `1492212534`, `1496274974`, `1506232604` |
| 2017 | Free tier becomes **3**; notes/journal; yearly frequency added "two days after I thought of it" | `1555344256`, `1531163399`, `1489194676` |
| 2018 | Groups (Mar), v1.6 widget (Apr, one report of deleted habits), Watch app, Dropbox backup; price $4.99 → $6.99 | `2301362573`, `2465703291`, `2588363191`, `2310415957` |
| Dec 2018 | **Growth Bundle** of sibling apps first appears | `3581875318` |
| 2019 | Price 6.49€ → 10.99€ → 7.99€ over New Year; Touch ID moved behind the paywall; journal and timestamp updates | `3597301586`, `3873301504`, `3877589027`, `3904790978` |
| **31 Aug 2020** | **One-time Pro removed. $19.99/yr for Done, $29.99/yr for the bundle** (Done alone costlier than the bundle via other apps) | `6380661176`, `6395873344`, `6388925380`, `6422603877` |
| Sep 2020 → Sep 2021 | iOS 14 home-screen widgets: Done has none; 73 widget reviews (16.2% of the window) | `6444616754`, `6701730862`, `6920318612`, `7310394855` |
| **14 Dec 2020** | **Ownership change** ("sold from Tree Betty to this new owner"). "Self-care Christmas" banner hides the bottom habit, including for paid users. Promo $11.99 → $39.99/yr | `6757212129`, `6738427534`, `6751801484`, `6758584064`, `6760348196` |
| 2021 | Perpetual countdown offers; from ~Jun 2021 the **bundle is the only upgrade path**; renewals at $59.99–$99.99/yr; reviewers identify the owner as Reflectly | `6837024394`, `7513649695`, `7565446472`, `7780326363`, `7023586896` |
| Oct 2021 | Old tappable widget replaced by a non-interactive one; intro screen freezes | `7862683254`, `7890317143`, `7874996576`, `7906461142` |
| **22 Dec 2021 → ~8 Jan 2022** | **v1.19.0 crash on launch** during a TikTok-driven install surge | `8184394543`, `8193092532`, `8200043639`, `8217777536` |
| Mar 2022 | **v2 rebuild**: 10–30 s tap lag, Watch app broken, Shortcuts & quit-to-zero removed, past-entry editing removed (restored in 2.0.8) | `8461640396`, `8463235133`, `8479953821`, `8608592456` |
| May–Jun 2022 | Hang on launch after an update; fixed ~10 June | `8739679081`, `8756283245`, `8758000773` |
| Sep 2022 | **v2.7.1 / 2.7.2** corrupt counts (58,720 runs in a week) | `9124745599`, `9126313490`, `9131616072` |
| Apr 2022 → 2023 | "83–90% off" first-year offers ($8.99–$9.99) that **check out at full price** ($59.99–$99.99) | `8557086081`, `9364358413`, `9533978350`, `9782364954` |
| Dec 2022 – Jan 2023 | **Monthly and yearly frequencies removed** | `9438711434`, `9452439761`, `9515055927` |
| **27 Jan 2023** | **v3.0 redesign**: streak off the list, swipe-between-days removed, forced daily/weekly grouping, week-start bug, custom count adds +1. Streak partly restored ~20 Feb | `9559928265`, `9564885641`, `9716393527`, `9764405661`, `9638198190` |
| **Apr 2023** | **Legacy one-time / lifetime Premium no longer recognised**; permanent "Just for you / A gift to yourself" banner; achievements added | `9833924285`, `9835947274`, `9839668950`, `9855591432` |
| **Aug 2023 → 2024** | **"Lifetime" offer at $19.99 / £19.99 / €22.99 / CA$24.99 / A$29.99 against anchors of $399–CA$499.99.** Purchases charge but do not unlock; buyers are routed into a "gift" Growth-Bundle upsell | `10319481379`, `10590258881`, `10618779175`, `10667674988`, `10639028732` |
| Jan 2024 | The offer shows **twice per launch** | `10912142470`, `10917111173`, `11002029170` |
| 2024 → 2026 | Chronic lag; Health counts doubled; developer replies begin to appear, with limited follow-through | `12068066761`, `11094536706`, `11834026398`, `14196021819` |
| ~Apr 2025 | App re-downloads as **"Do"**; a lifetime buyer is asked to pay again | `12530647942`, `12731608760` |
| 2026 | Notification-permission prompt on every open; habit count capped at 366 on the list | `13598734047`, `13613089520`, `13795607364` |

## 2.3 What is gated, and the price ladder

**The gate itself barely changed; its price and packaging did.** From 2017 onward the free tier is **3 habits**, stated by 670 reviews in every year. A minority report 2 (`8297234145`, `9782836503`, `10483651299`, `11014993576`) or 4–5 (`9487564673`, `10771974494`, `13645023878`), usually after deleting and re-adding habits, which the free tier does not always allow (`10438843029`, `12131683960`). Multiple reminders, groups and the passcode were also gated in E1.

| Period | What an upgrade cost, per reviewers | Representative IDs |
|---|---|---|
| 2016–2017 | £2.99 / $3.99 / 3.99€ one-time | `1396394692`, `1582140172`, `1658095535` |
| 2018 | $4.99 / £4.99 / CA$6.99 / 22 TL one-time | `2588363191`, `3222885967`, `2310415957`, `3029183675` |
| 2019 – Aug 2020 | $6.99 → $8.99; 7.99€ – 10.99€; A$10.99; CA$9.99 – 12 one-time | `3634730640`, `3597301586`, `3749138405`, `5906389618`, `6229936421` |
| Sep 2020 | $19.99/yr (Done); $29.99/yr (bundle) | `6395873344`, `6433945962` |
| Dec 2020 – 2021 | $11.99 intro → $39.99/yr; CA$53/yr; £38/yr; A$65/yr | `6760348196`, `6841721045`, `6885504701`, `6990650128` |
| mid-2021 – 2023 | Bundle only: $59.99 – $99.99/yr (€100.99; CA$73.99; £51.99; A$86.99; 639 kr) with "83–90% off" first years of $8.99 – $15.99 | `7780326363`, `7936506294`, `9441999855`, `9450702497`, `9175246401`, `8928443111` |
| Aug 2023 – 2024 | "Lifetime": $19.99 / £19.99 / €22.99 / CA$24.99 / A$29.99 / ₹1,999, against anchors of $399 – CA$499.99 / 450€ / ₹39,999; also $79.99 lifetime | `10319481379`, `10323628736`, `10590258881`, `10846859058`, `11443853483`, `11323900370` |
| 2025–2026 | ~$60/yr, £52/yr, 62€/yr, 69€/yr, A$90/yr; annual only | `12405730052`, `13014797511`, `13465804405`, `12652123252`, `13423556414` |

**Interpretation (from dated price mentions):**
- The *effective* entry price rose roughly 15× in recurring terms: from a one-off $3.99 to $59.99 per year.
- The *advertised* price became unmoored from the charged price. The corpus contains 69 reviews where the offer shown was not the price charged or quoted at checkout (Part 4.3).
- Price objection tracks the model change, not the cap: 4.1% of E1 → 13.0% of E2. The cap complaint was 13.5% in E1 and 14.0% in E4 — essentially unchanged.

## 2.4 Signals about the developer and ownership

**The E1 developer was visibly responsive.**
- A feature shipped two days after a reviewer wished for it (`1489194676`).
- A real person replied to a crash email and shipped a fix "ASAP" (`1506232604`).
- A purchase-restore request was answered "within minutes" (`2349426990`).
- A suggested unit-count feature shipped "almost immediately" (`5128094125`).
- Others: `4471880868`, `6072294655` ("devs listen to issues and suggestions"), `6089547076`.

**After Dec 2020, reviewers describe a changed operator:**
- the app "was sold from Tree Betty to this new owner", and support links went unchanged (`6757212129`);
- `7023586896` names Reflectly and credits the original solo creator;
- "ever since the original designer/developer sold it … it's stopped working" (`9223896356`); also `8164542220`, `9890673197`;
- `6929556009` and `6950911208` link Done to the developers of "Habit" and Reflectly.

The current metadata developer is Kodeon, Inc. **No review mentions Kodeon.** 28 reviews (0.54%, emerging) discuss the ownership change.

**Developer replies to reviews appear only from 2024**, and the reviews that mention them are split:
- **Resolved:** `11834026398` (Premium restored "within a couple days"), `12495653099` (restored 11 days after re-sending proof of purchase), `10286924890` (lifetime fixed by a named support agent), `9476288002` (a support agent helped cancel).
- **Unresolved:** `11743876580` (asked to use a single email address), `12473833371` (promised refund never issued), `13602037395` ("they sneakily changed their developer response"), `14196021819`, `13660661446`.

**Every one of the fifteen worst-rated themes is about money-handling, support, or the app failing to run. No feature gap appears in this list.** Note also that the free-tier cap, the most-cited monetisation complaint, has a far milder rating profile (mean 2.01) than the purchase-integrity themes (mean 1.14–1.39). **Interpretation:** people tolerate a gate; they do not tolerate paying for a gate that doesn't open.

## 3.3 Reading the monetisation objection correctly

Separating the 1,659 monetisation-friction reviews by what they actually argue (manual reading; overlapping; qualitative split, not additional quantified themes):

| Argument | Rough share | Example IDs |
|---|---|---|
| "Three habits isn't enough to *use* or *judge* a habit app" | the plurality | `1885852147` ("I would like to start by tracking 5 habits, not 3, to see what things really make sense"), `5692999560` ("Free is useless, paid is too expensive to try"), `8201969049`, `9467534091`, `10788092145` |
| "A subscription for a checklist is unjustifiable" (value) | large from E2 | `6541452186`, `8995559986` ("That's almost the price of Disney+!"), `9529283208` ("In this economy?"), `10590258881` ("How is this app worth $499.99?"), `13423556414` ("$90/pY for what?") |
| "I'd pay once, never a subscription" | steady from E2 | `6433945962`, `7078419492`, `7773592605`, `9021968448`, `10109957362` |
| "I cannot afford it" (constraint; many are teens) | ~110 teen/low-income | `8031917452` ("your target market at this point is us teenagers"), `8655024880` (14), `8861031530` (12), `9459269072` (11), `9540681367` (mental-health, can't work) |
| "Self-improvement shouldn't be paywalled" (principle) | frequent in 2022–23 | `9465306733` ("how dare I expect a self care app to help me with self care?"), `8222093807` (therapist-recommended, blocked at habit 4), `10121153980` (ADHD) |
| "Make it paid up front, stop pretending it's free" | ~57 disclosure | `3690855663`, `5736170964`, `6541452186`, `8876627532`, `10912142470` |

**Against these sit 119 price-praise reviews** (mean 3.82). They are concentrated in E1 (63, 6.7% of E1) and include the clearest statement of what the old model sold: "I'm only paying to support the developers" (`1860418284`), "a one-time charge is a lot less exploitative, and doesn't come with the same guilt" (`3441366071`), "Best $8.99 spent in a long time!" (`5885951702`). **27 E1 reviews (mean 4.78) say one-time pricing was the reason they chose Done.**

**Interpretation:** the cap was acceptable when the unlock was a $4–9 one-off that reviewers read as fair. The same cap became the front door to an annual subscription that reviewers read as unjustified for a "checklist". It was then further poisoned by fake-urgency offers and purchases that did not apply. A reader should not conclude "lower the price" alone. The evidence says **restore a trustworthy one-time or low-commitment path, and make it actually work.**

## 3.4 What the product genuinely does well

| Strength | n (4–5★) | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Simple, uncluttered, fast to log (when fast) | 458 | `1525119279` ("track my habits with a single tap … make my habit data easy to see"), `4730795646` ("The user discovers value rather than have thousands of distractions flung at him"), `11121493895`, `12220855265`, `13701503325` |
| Beautiful, colourful, "friendly" design | 250 | `2564271918` (compares Habit-Bull's "roided-out CrossFit guy" with Done's friendliness), `2348669818`, `5546153028`, `9604671744` |
| Motivation through bars filling, streaks and sounds | 200 | `2591717963` ("an adult sticker chart!"), `3675112801`, `5255921582`, `10203803324` |
| Build and quit in one model | 96 mentions | `1515456440`, `3304229802`, `6624535423`, `5554448272` |
| "N times per week", not fixed days | 81 mentions | `4191961911`, `4879659323` (monthly view prevents "feel bad" spirals), `11030718864`, `9260524824` |
| Units and custom increments | — | `3752603970`, `5128094125`, `6053003661` (dollar amounts for business referrals), `9245650288` |
| Health and medical routines | 36 medical | `1493272441` (doctors and therapists), `5950318415` (hip-surgery physio), `6100935111` (Ménière's), `6785378270` (95-day medication streak), `9560996028` |
| Life outcomes | 113 | `7462019995` (34 kg), `8190335385` (15 lb), `5968630345`, `3191380653`, `9115655144` |
| ADHD / neurodivergent fit | 33 (mean 4.09) | `3532244458`, `8101391015` ("every other … app overcomplicates … this app is BEAUTIFUL and simple"), `8562572307`, `10358657498`, `12731608760`, `14485083676` |
| Coaches and clinicians recommend it | 4 | `5165967299` (coaching clients), `6252857296` (clarity coach), `7439931340` (psychology graduate student), `8222093807` (therapist) |

**Broken or regressed — the capability existed and stopped working (Part 4):** app launch, entitlement, Watch app, sync / backup / restore, Health counts, widget interactivity, streak display, monthly frequency, swipe, quit-to-zero, Shortcuts, week start, time zones, custom-count input, export.

**Misunderstandings (a UX problem, not a feature gap):**
- how to edit or delete a habit (`2207075197`, `4072588088`, `5670621380`, `8342232403`);
- what "untracked" means (`9594936504`, `13672019176`);
- what colours and X/O marks mean (`7460675158`, `8589113811`, `9030043954`);
- how "quit" goals count (`3760248631`, `3846513908`, `4030848224`, `7800629578`).

## 3.6 Competitors named in the corpus

**241 reviews (4.65%)** name a competitor or say they "tried them all". **Direction flips after 2020.**
- **E1 — people arrive from elsewhere** (89 reviews, 9.5% of E1): from Productive (`2405289516` "$5/month??? Have they lost their minds???", `4378411545`, `5407501811`, `6229936421`), Streaks (`1493502607`, `3653996291`), Habitify (`3685937182`), Habit-Bull / Way of Life / Productivity (`2564271918`), Strides (`3878741405`), Lazy Bones (`3015459317`).
- **E2–E5 — people leave for elsewhere:** Streaks (`6723043562`, `8756283245`, `10118088583`, `13036517547` "Streaks is a one-time purchase and is equal if not better"), Habit (`7851177861`, `8196831910`, `8202651237`, `10392159256`, `10909221224`), Habit Tracker (`8208926052`, `9613687854`, `10433351066`), Way of Life (`6388925380`), Productive (`6161986689`, `10635102760`), DayStamp (`8755202103`), Onrise (`11101822527`), Habitify (`10836281554`), Grit (`13660661446`), Finch (`9460391109`, `9465013532`, `10777220901`), Daylio (`7751088617`), Structured (`9557003343`), smile todo (`8204982481`).

Siblings named: Tally, Do, Grateful, Moody, Last, Time Block, Reflectly (`5835754604`, `5948549297`, `7901290433`).

# PART 4 — THE FIVE CLUSTERS THAT EXPLAIN THE CORPUS

## 4.1 Cluster 1 — The launch-failure waves `[high-priority globally; concentrated]`

**1,052 reviews (20.29%), mean 1.30, 884 of them 1★.** By year: 2016 4 · 2017 2 · 2018 1 · 2019 2 · 2020 2 · 2021 118 · **2022 892** · 2023 21 · 2024 5 · 2025 4 · 2026 1.

| Wave | Window | Symptom | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| Launch season | Nov–Dec 2016 | Freeze on adding a first habit | `1490108215`, `1492212534`, `1496274974`, `1496737022`, `1497665944`, `1501609542`; fixed per `1506232604` |
| Intro freeze | Oct–Dec 2021 | Stuck on tutorial ("Tap the O"), can't get past onboarding | `7874996576`, `7882392404`, `7888565410`, `7898872462`, `7906461142`, `7922382626`, `7955655172`, `8012895843` |
| **v1.19.0 crash on launch** | **22 Dec 2021 → ~8 Jan 2022** | Logo flashes, app closes, "takes me back to my home screen" | 918 reviews in the window; `8184394543`, `8193092532`, `8200043639`, `8204886004`, `8189189742`, `8194223386` ("using the full version … since 2015"), `8211970729` ("used it everyday for hundreds of days") |
| Post-update hang | 30 May → ~10 Jun 2022 | White / blank screen after restore from iCloud | `8732272480`, `8739679081`, `8747212957`, `8754506476`, `8755202103`, `8756283245`, `8758000773` |
| Freeze after subscribing | Dec 2022 → 2023 | App freezes on adding a habit or after purchase | `9419785982`, `9426943676`, `9439567941`, `9445167474`, `9455182130`, `10003065436` |

**The 1.19.0 wave in numbers:**
- **1,135 reviews in 25 days (21.89% of the whole corpus)**, mean 1.44, **79.4% 1★**;
- **918 launch failures**, 786 of them 1★;
- **51% from outside the US**: gb 118, ca 100, br 31, mx 25, de 21, pe 19, co 19, au 18, be 16, za 15 …;
- TikTok named in 57 wave reviews; 47 reviewers say they never got to use the app ("I can't even rate this properly because I can't even get into the app" `8196695273`);
- devices named range from iPhone 6/6S/7/8 to iPhone 11/12/12 Pro Max, so not an old-device issue (`8172114045`, `8197301083`, `8197352881`, `8199164061`, `8200538336`, `8207454012`).

**Fix:** reviewers report that updating then reinstalling worked from about 7–9 January (`8213743084`, `8217777536`, `8223042075`, `8229706176`).

**Collateral — data loss:** `8189189742` ("Now the app will open, but it lost all my data!"), `8218402912` ("Lost more than 2 years of data"), `8263579754` ("my entire year worth of data has been wiped").

**Interpretation:**
- A crash on launch shipped at the exact moment a paid-acquisition or viral campaign was driving installs.
- The corpus cannot establish causation between the two.
- **The coincidence converted the app's largest-ever audience into its largest-ever pile of 1★ reviews: 35% of lifetime 1★ in 25 days.**
- The 2022 mean of 1.74 is therefore substantially a release-engineering outcome, not a product-concept verdict.

## 4.2 Cluster 2 — The paywall and upsell architecture `[high-priority]`

**Monetisation friction union: 1,659 (31.99%), mean 1.90.** Components:
- free cap 670 (12.92%);
- upsell nag 676 (13.04%);
- price objection 379 (7.31%);
- general paywall 364 (7.02%);
- countdown 111 (2.14%);
- one-time-removed objection 106 (2.04%);
- price not disclosed / misleading "free" 57 (1.10%);
- bundle-only upgrade 36 (0.69%).

**(a) The cap.** It is present in all eras at 8–18%. The E1 version was 2 items (2016) and then 3. Reviewers repeatedly name the evaluation problem: "Ready and willing to pay, but I'd first like to try it more. With only 2 items makes it hard to get a feel" (`1455609889`, 2016). A minority defend it: `2018738389` ("it made me prioritise"), `2768136872` ("if you've read *The One Thing*, three is already a lot!"), `5443554210` ("two (three, actually) habits at a time seem like the perfect amount"), `8609009259` ("3 goal tracking … is plenty for me"), `10034330248`.

**(b) Upsell escalation.** Upsell share rose from 1.9% (E1) to 14.9% (E2), 9.9% (E3) and 22.6% (E4). The mechanisms reviewers name:
1. **Banner that hides a habit** (Dec 2020): `6751801484`, `6753490588`, `6758584064`, `6760348196`.
2. **Full-screen offer on every launch** (2021 →): `6876625802`, `6945830732`, `7007819440`, `7124882010`, `8722563354`, `9942976990`.
3. **Countdown that resets** — 111 reviews: `6837024394` ("72 hour countdown and reset back to 72 hrs"), `6953163730` ("like the DFS adverts, constantly on sale"), `7710455837` ("ran for months"), `10817420229`, `11443853483`.
4. **Permanent "Just for you" list cell** (Apr 2023) that reviewers say slows scrolling and tapping: `9819638240`, `9837113919`, `9837641197`, `9839487554`, `10001218229`.
5. **Two pop-ups per launch** (Jan 2024 →), 19 reviews: `10880872919`, `10912142470` ("I'm pretty sure this type of fake sale is actually illegal"), `10917111173`, `11002029170`, `11014993576`.
6. **Upsell to paying users** — 314 reviews: `6759066974`, `8558704853`, `8753896563`, `8860030136` ("It's not encouraging customer loyalty!!"), `9338298616` (mood-disorder tracking interrupted), `9372242891`, `10009036826`, `11434900723`.

**(c) Bundle as the only path** (mid-2021 →), 36 reviews: "There is no way to only upgrade this app to premium" (`7699793759`), `7513649695`, `7563935878`, `7815166558` ("the renewal jumps up to 10x the price in a year's time"), `9967933638`, `11585169439`.

**(d) Disclosure.** 57 reviews say the price was hidden or the listing implied "free" (`3690855663` "needs an in-app purchases mark", `5736170964`, `6207964476`, `6541452186`, `8210236272`, `8606066995` "online ads … said it was under $10 one time"). A further 50 describe an intro price renewing much higher:
- `7780326363`: 9.99€ → 100.99€ "only in the small print";
- `9450702497`: £5.99 → £51.99, "just hoping someone doesn't cancel";
- `9400136347`: had to switch to monthly to be able to cancel before a €100+ renewal;
- also `9509987159`, `10504270286`.

**(e) Accessibility of the offer.** Teens and low-income reviewers (110) are a visible segment of the 2022–23 TikTok cohort, and the cap's position lands hardest on them (`8024387770`, `8031917452`, `9465416666`, `10729845467`).

## 4.3 Cluster 3 — Billing and entitlement integrity `[high-priority]`

**768 reviews (14.81%), mean 1.30, 637 of them 1★.** By era: **1.6% → 8.4% → 5.2% → 34.5% → 25.4%.**

**(a) Paid Premium not granted / not honoured — 468 (9.02%), mean 1.23.** By year: 2020 11 · 2021 12 · 2022 10 · **2023 200 · 2024 200** · 2025 27 · 2026 2. Payers are 304 of the 468.

| Sub-cluster | n | Mechanism described | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Legacy purchase revoked / not restorable** | 189 | 2016–2021 one-time or "lifetime" purchase shows as "Done Basic" after an update, reinstall or new phone; "Restore purchase" says restored and nothing changes, or "Oops, we couldn't find a purchase to restore" | `2249883071` (2018, first report), `6781827609`, `7409688659`, `9833924285` ("scammed me out of my lifetime premium plan … writing the most trafficked review article"), `9835947274`, `9839668950`, `9863669849` ("Customer support … told me they wouldn't honour my previous purchase"), `9869035157` (reply "full of emojis", offered a free trial), `9940569707`, `10430541861`, `10489995181` ("$3.99 and gave me 'access for life'"), `11505353898` ("grandfathered in … After that they removed that grandfathered access"), `13465804405`, `14196021819` |
| **2023+ offer purchase never applied** | 295 | Charged $19.99–$29.99 for "lifetime"; app stays Basic; the same offer keeps appearing; after purchase a "gift" screen pushes the Growth Bundle, and dismissing it drops the user back to Basic | `10319481379`, `10324957727`, `10333454546` (retiree, detailed), `10391681656` (bought "lifetime" repeatedly), `10413198433`, `10639028732`, `10667674988` ("The gift is … other 99$ for a yearly subscription"), `10695671832` ("by not 'finishing' this flow and purchasing the upsell, I think it doesn't recognize I purchased it"), `11393421407` (premium only appeared after *accidentally* subscribing to the bundle), `11653391592`, `11689173519` ("my plan shows up as 'Done basic'"), `11727458734` |
| Resolved cases | 5+ | Support restored after proof of purchase | `8639856009` (a year later), `10286924890`, `11834026398`, `12495653099`, `6585701849` (bundle showed after an hour) |

**(b) Discount offers checked out at full price — 69 (1.33%), mean 1.58; 49 say they were actually charged.** By year: 2020 2 · **2022 29 · 2023 35** · 2024 3. First reports: `6765423786` and `6771135614` (Dec 2020, "71% discount … still charged the full amount"). Peak: `8557086081` ("It had the $59.99 crossed out and a price next to it of $9.99 … My card was charged full price"), `9124637338`, `9175246401` (A$15.99 → A$86.99), `9364358413`, `9444835004`, `9450067550` (support "told me … it's a bug that happens sometimes"), `9533978350`, `9782364954`, `9845942388`, `10016313150`, `10062204698`. Others saw the full price at checkout and stopped: `9069674471`, `9584426315`, `9669256511`, `9691919304`, `10191641235`, `10205175543`.

**(c) Cannot cancel / charged after cancelling — 66 (1.27%), mean 1.39.** Mostly E4 (43). Reviewers:
- cannot find a subscription in iOS Settings to cancel (`9798455172`, `10771826228`, `11870900851`);
- were billed after cancelling (`10366403685`, `11594644730`, `12131407578`, `12263878766` "cancelled … TWICE and I'm still being charged");
- could not cancel before a renewal (`9509987159`, `9400136347`).

One reviewer claims charges ran outside Apple's subscription list (`10323742685`), and one claims recurring charges for three years on a lifetime purchase (`11443832766`). Both are unverified single claims.

**(d) Billing-dispute language — 403 (7.77%).** This is a broader lexical theme: refund, charged, scam, fraud, *estafa*, *arnaque*, *Abzocke*. It includes 13 reviews explicitly calling for Apple to remove the developer (`10795036200` "we all should report a problem … select 'Scam or Fraud'", `11101822527`).

**Interpretation:**
- Every mechanism here sits in the purchase → entitlement → upsell code path, and the timing tracks product decisions visible in 2.2.
- Legacy failures begin at the Dec 2020 ownership change, spike in Apr 2023 and continue into 2026.
- New-offer failures begin exactly when the $19.99 lifetime offer launched (Aug 2023).
- The corpus cannot say whether the defect was deliberate, negligent or technical. It can say it ran for **at least 16 months with 200 reviews a year** describing it.

## 4.4 Cluster 4 — Reliability and data trust after the rebuilds `[high-priority]`

**Freeze / lag — 404 (7.79%), mean 1.62.** Share by era: 3.1% → 5.4% → 8.0% → 9.0% → **26.6%**. Reviewers name specific latencies:
- "10 seconds for the sound effect, another 20 seconds for the colour to change" (`8478699114`, Mar 2022);
- "I now have to wait for more than 10 seconds for each item … habits I've been doing for more than 600 days" (`8461640396`);
- "it now takes several seconds to add a single entry … apparently can't cope with too many entries" (`12066404983`, iPhone 15 Pro);
- "20-30 seconds per habit" (`12068066761`), "45 sec" (`12299152685`), "freezes up for about 30 seconds" (`12725571465`);
- also `13808211870`, `14116063088`, `14121729788`.

A distinct 2023 claim ties lag to the promotional banner (`9819638240`, `9839487554`, `10001218229`) and to "throttling" non-subscribers (`9902067482`, `9836149317`). **That is a reviewer inference, not an established fact.**

**Data integrity:**

| Problem | n | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Data / streaks lost or reset | 94 | `2465703291` (v1.6), `3870016780`, `7377929999` (109-day no-wine streak), `8263579754`, `8583843842` (sobriety 40 → 16 days), `9130172390` ("second time"), `10775645224` (200+ day streaks to 0), `12510594893`, `12074274125` |
| Counts recorded wrongly (incl. v2.7.x) | 107 | `8711658182` (1,822.8 miles), `9126313490` (2,622 runs by Tuesday), `9128448532` (135,546), `9131616072` (58,720 — "Do not download 2.7.2!!!"), `9168682961` (58,235 miles), `9694337598`, `10168205264` ("forgets the data I entered half the time") |
| Apple Health counts wrong | 37 | `9123656067` (2.7.1 added a year of steps), `10628437249` (sleep 14 h), `11094536706` (steps and meditation doubled), `12004489241` (26,000 vs 5,000 steps), `12031381895`, `12773578933`, `13613981255` |
| Time-zone travel rewrites streaks | 13 | `3323964863`, `9550024629`, `9998476908` (600+ days), `11133834669` (1,500+ days), `11377557606`, `14489049875` |
| Week-start setting ignored (resets Sunday) | 30 | `8016698258` (widget), `8246605058`, `9668372318` (premium user of 3 years, "begging"), `9716393527`, `9811370838`, `9828604789`, `9852534354` |
| Custom-count input adds +1 | 16 | `9764405661` ("I type in 50 pages … it counts 55"), `9788751933`, `9852534354`, `9862437790`, `8885948087` |
| Apple Watch app broken / out of sync | 60 | `2713622777` (2018), `8482375818`, `8497681706`, `8516201327`, `8564032938`, `8636700518` (support asked for a video, then silence), `9260807388` ("'update coming soon'. 6 months later"), `11944559703`, `12248298278` |
| Sync / backup / restore fails | 83 | `5395293570` ("Manual syncing is so 2010"), `7516130965`, `7525700298`, `8733984482`, `9750752573`, `12283528605`, `13265256358` |
| Export hangs | — | `9600503059`, `13620635669`, `14121729788` ("I can't export my data to move to another app because it hangs on the CSV export") |

**Interpretation:** a habit tracker's only durable asset is the user's history. `8583843842` (sobriety), `9560996028` and `9561693468` (medication), `6785378270` and `13966079285` (47-month streak) show that users treat that history as health data. Every item in this table erodes the switching cost that would otherwise protect the product. The export hang (`14121729788`) turns that into a lock-in complaint.

## 4.5 Cluster 5 — Features removed by redesigns `[very strong in 2023]`

**Feature-removal union: 192 (3.70%) — 9.35% of E4. An update is blamed for a regression in 215 reviews (4.15%).**

| Removed | n | When | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Monthly & yearly goal frequency** | 58 | Dec 2022 – Jan 2023 | `9438711434` ("This was the ONE reason I used to love this app"), `9452439761`, `9515055927`, `9526250306` (contact lenses monthly, weight loss yearly), `9564490880` ("call friend once a month or take a vacation three times a year"), `10358657498` (ADHD; deep-clean the oven monthly), `11331485150` ("goals are all about the long term"), `13624483599` (still asked in Jan 2026) |
| **Streak count on the main list** | 52 | v3.0, 27 Jan 2023; partly restored ~20 Feb; removed again Aug 2023 per `10266641762` | `9557519480`, `9559928265`, `9560996028` (30-day pill cycle), `9561693468` (medication), `9564394889`, `9585668347` ("Please don't respond by telling me I can see that data with 'a few simple clicks'"), `9622188814`; fixed: `9638198190` |
| **Swipe between days** | 41 | v3.0 | `9559832988`, `9566841290`, `9576220955`, `9637271261`, `10947045135`, `11518468921`, `11690220252` |
| Quit-to-zero goals | — | Mar 2022 and Jan 2023 | `8468652583`, `8479953821` (sobriety tracking), `8585211667`, `9544281409` |
| Siri Shortcuts | — | Mar 2022 | `8463235133` (automations broke), `8498726949`, `9402843260`, `9479023592` |
| Tappable Today-view widget | — | Oct 2021 | `7862683254`, `7890317143`, `7964981568`, `8055151737` (memory disability, "requesting a refund"), `8718896246`, `8792407471`, `9050514368` (detailed) |
| Custom order / forced grouping | 22 | 2023 | `9689878548`, `9568715481`, `10441359998`, `13467130073` |
| "Last done / minutes ago"; day-start time | — | 2022–2023 | `8724502286`, `8770030841`, `9532238765`, `9540594680` |
| Editing past entries | — | v2.0.2 → restored in 2.0.8 | `8486528411`, `8608592456`, `8688332336` |

**The sharpest statement of the pattern** is `8724502286`, an edited log across the v2 era: "devastating 2.0.c updates … slowed the app and broke the watch app … this week's update finally restore the watch app after months … Unfortunately, the recent updates have dropped 'minutes ago'…". A second is `9532238765`: "The app gets prettier. The actual functionality gets worse. Every time." (`9544281409` says the same).

**A minority like the redesigns:** `9604671744` ("the app's new owners are choosing to evolve the product" — positive), `9531796562`, `9637363785`, `10947045135`.

# PART 5 — RATINGS ANALYSIS

The corpus is 1★-dominant (49.6%). Each band is a different population with different complaints.

## 5.1 5★ — n = 976 (18.82% of corpus)

| Theme | n | % of 5★ |
|---|---|---|
| Simplicity / ease | 358 | 36.7% |
| Design praised | 171 | 17.5% |
| Motivation / streaks | 168 | 17.2% |
| Life outcome | 102 | 10.5% |
| **Monetisation friction** | **96** | **9.8%** |
| Customisation | 87 | 8.9% |
| Competitor comparison | 84 | 8.6% |
| Price praised | 69 | 7.1% |
| Stats | 48 | 4.9% |
| Build + quit | 42 | 4.3% |
| One-time pricing praised | 21 | 2.2% |
| ADHD named | 21 | 2.2% |

**Two sub-populations inside 5★:**
- **E1 enthusiasts (504 of the 976 are from 2016–2020).** Long, comparative reviews that name features, price and developer: `2348669818`, `2463611317`, `2564271918`, `3441366071`, `5094171573`, `6624535423`.
- **Later loyalists** who love the same core and say so despite everything: `8101391015`, `10358657498`, `11121493895`, `12220855265`, `13701503325`, `14485083676`.

**Some 5★ reviews are not praise.** About 1–2% of 5★ are complaints rated 5 "so people see it" (`8174958442`, `8283676574`, `10323742685`) or "won't open" reports (`8192754596`, `8202718494`). This does not change any finding.

## 5.2 4★ — n = 452 (8.72%)

| Theme | n | % of 4★ |
|---|---|---|
| Simplicity | 100 | 22.1% |
| **Monetisation friction** | **98** | **21.7%** |
| Design | 79 | 17.5% |
| Reliability (union) | 60 | 13.3% |
| **Widget mention** | **57** | **12.6%** |
| Free cap | 47 | 10.4% |
| **Platform request (iPad / Mac / landscape)** | **34** | **7.5%** |
| Stats | 27 | 6.0% |
| Feature removal | 28 | 6.2% |
| Localisation | 14 | 3.1% |

4★ is the **feature-request band**:
- the widget peaks here (12.6% vs 3.8% globally);
- platform requests peak here (7.5% vs 1.9%);
- localisation reaches 3.1% (vs 1.2%).

These are engaged users naming the missing piece between 4 and 5: "I will gladly give 5 stars" with an iPad layout (`4923521854`), a widget (`7031530229`), a streak freeze (`4492148493`), Health sync (`4420531543`), grouped categories (`2961596140`).

## 5.3 3★ — n = 581 (11.20%)

| Theme | n | % of 3★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Monetisation friction** | **256** | **44.1%** |
| Free cap | 131 | 22.5% |
| Reliability | 129 | 22.2% |
| Upsell nag | 91 | 15.7% |
| Simplicity | 84 | 14.5% |
| Upsell to payers | 44 | 7.6% |
| Feature removal | 40 | 6.9% |

3★ is the **"good app, wrong gate / wrong update" band**:
- gate: `1885852147`, `3252526505`, `5754625203`, `9467534091`;
- update: `9557519480`, `9562255131`, `9621516200`;
- upsell after paying: `8836702535`, `8853131560`, `10236336525`.

## 5.4 2★ — n = 606 (11.69%)

| Theme | n | % of 2★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Monetisation friction** | **303** | **50.0%** |
| Reliability | 178 | 29.4% |
| Free cap | 133 | 21.9% |
| Upsell nag | 118 | 19.5% |
| Price objection | 85 | 14.0% |
| Launch failure | 75 | 12.4% |
| Billing integrity | 66 | 10.9% |
| Support unresponsive | 55 | 9.1% |
| Upsell to payers | 51 | 8.4% |
| Entitlement failure | 45 | 7.4% |

Half of 2★ is money. The 2★ band also holds the most detailed "I used to love this" long-tenure reviews: `6752713662`, `9591160875`, `9604308889` ("Notice that all of the rave reviews are from 1-4 years ago"), `10406832617`, `11690220252`.

## 5.5 1★ — n = 2,571 (49.58%)

| Theme | n | % of 1★ |
|---|---|---|
| **Reliability (union)** | **1,156** | **45.0%** |
| **Monetisation friction** | **906** | **35.2%** |
| **Launch failure** | **884** | **34.4%** |
| **Billing integrity** | **637** | **24.8%** |
| Explicit payer | 443 | 17.2% |
| Upsell nag | 412 | 16.0% |
| **Entitlement failure** | **399** | **15.5%** |
| **Support unresponsive** | **369** | **14.4%** |
| Billing-dispute language | 349 | 13.6% |
| Free cap | 324 | 12.6% |
| Freeze / lag | 267 | 10.4% |

**What drives the 1★ band:**
- **Launch failure** accounts for a third of all 1★.
- **Money-handling** (billing integrity) accounts for a quarter.
- **17.2% of 1★ reviews come from people who say they paid.** A meaningful share of the worst ratings are failed customers, not freeloaders.

The 1★ band by era is two different complaints:
- **E3 1★ (n = 1,274): 66.4% are launch failures** (846).
- **E4 1★ (n = 924): 52.4% are billing and entitlement integrity** (484), and 38.1% are entitlement failures specifically (352).

The rhetorical register matters for store-page conversion. It includes sustained fraud accusations in eight languages: `9197717753` ("Billing Scam"), `10145697472`, `10618779175` ("Criminals!"), `11724691661`, `13439321031` ("欺诈"), `10323325986` ("¡Que estafa!"), `9845959262` ("Gierig nach Geld!"), `10366403685` ("Arnaque"). There are also explicit calls on Apple to remove the app (`10795036200`, `11101822527`, `11020907850`).

## 5.6 Themes that cut across the rating line

| Theme | 5★+4★ | 3★ | 2★+1★ | Reading |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Free cap | 82 | 131 | 457 | Hostile, but one in eight comes from people rating 4–5★ |
| Simplicity | 458 | 84 | 183 | The top praise; also used against the price ("a simple app shouldn't cost…") |
| Design | 250 | 69 | 135 | Same pattern |
| Widget | 103 | 32 | 60 | Positive in E1; demand in E2; complaint after Oct 2021 |
| Competitor named | 109 | 28 | 104 | Arrivals (E1) vs departures (E2+) |
| Payer | 56 | 56 | 513 | Paying customers now mostly write when something breaks |
| Price praise | 81 | 6 | 32 | Net positive, and it disappears after E1 |

**The single most important cross-cutting fact:** the qualities that earn 5★ — simplicity, colour, sounds, streaks — are also quoted in the 1★ band, as a reason the app is worth using *and* as a reason a subscription is unjustified. The product concept is not what failed.

# PART 6 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 6.1 Framing this correctly

| Group | Definition | n | % of corpus | Mean |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Explicit payers** | First-person "I paid / bought / purchased / upgraded / subscribed", or "premium member / paying customer" | **625** | 12.05% | **1.62** |
| Payers with a legacy hint | …and say the purchase was years ago / one-time / before the subscription | 134 | 2.58% | — |
| Global | All reviews | 5,186 | 100% | 2.355 |

**No conversion rate is claimed, and none can be claimed from review text.**

## 6.2 The headline: payers went from the happiest segment to the angriest

| Era | Explicit payers | % of era | Mean |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 (2016 – Aug 2020) | 38 | 4.0% | **3.76** |
| E2 (Sep 2020 – Dec 2021) | 44 | 7.7% | **1.93** |
| E3 (2022) | 98 | 5.3% | **1.80** |
| E4 (2023–24) | 409 | **25.0%** | **1.35** |
| E5 (2025–26) | 36 | 20.8% | 1.58 |

**Payer mean fell 2.41 points from E1 to E4, while payers' share of reviews rose six-fold.** In 2023–24 one review in four is written by someone who says they paid — and their mean is 1.35. **Interpretation:** in the E1 era people wrote to recommend the purchase. In E4 they wrote because the purchase did not work.

## 6.3 What made people buy

| Trigger | Evidence |
|---|---|
| **One-time price, explicitly *not* a subscription** (E1) | `2322753612`, `2405289516`, `2871629506` ("happily paid the one-time fee because many other programs are switching to a subscription"), `3441366071`, `5368023814`, `6229936421` |
| Tried the 3 free habits and wanted more | `3011906416` ("didn't hesitate to upgrade after playing with the free version … for a few hours"), `3030057042`, `3143895164`, `5358284870` |
| Charts and data once unlocked | `4815348275` ("excited to see them … upgraded immediately"), `3121672110`, `4392697150` (export to Google Drive) |
| Supporting a small team | `1406222328` ("happy to support the designers"), `1860418284`, `4311052841` ("there is a small team behind so I was happy to pay") |
| Deep discount / "one-year for $9.99" (E3–E4) | `7690833503`, `9395994303` (Tourette & OCD, "$10 instead of $60"), `9469085028`, `10624304018` (Black Friday) |
| "Lifetime for $19.99" (E4) | `10333454546`, `11544732240` (worked; see 6.5), `11121493895` |
| **To make the pop-up go away** (E3–E4) | `8346060268`, `9328333005` ("to get rid of the incessant pestering"), `10305533738`, `10935395497`, `11374154452`, `11315009912` |

**The last row is the corpus's clearest warning sign.** A share of E4 purchases were bought as relief from the upsell rather than for value. When the relief did not arrive (6.5), the result was the angriest reviews in the corpus.

## 6.4 What buyers value once they have paid

Unlimited habits and multiple reminders (`3014141825`, `2283332022`); charts (`4815348275`); groups (`2283332022`); export (`4392697150`); no ads and no account needed (`2461257805`, `4311052841`); and — for E1 buyers — the absence of any further ask (`3332657537` "love that they don't prompt you to update every 3 clicks").

## 6.5 What goes wrong after payment — ranked by severity

| Problem | n (within 625 payers) | % of payers | Mean | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Purchase not granted / not honoured** | **304** | **48.6%** | **1.21** | Part 4.3 |
| …2023+ offer never applied | 190 | 30.4% | 1.12 | `10324957727`, `10639028732`, `11689173519` |
| …legacy purchase revoked | 125 | 20.0% | 1.34 | `9835947274`, `10489995181`, `11505353898` |
| Support unresponsive | 214 | 34.2% | 1.25 | `10430541861`, `10623891834` |
| **Still shown upsells after paying** | **197** | **31.5%** | 1.51 | `8558704853`, `8860030136`, `10009036826` |
| Billing-dispute language | 161 | 25.8% | 1.18 | `9124637338`, `10902742038` |
| Freeze / lag after paying | 83 | 13.3% | 1.33 | `10052371902`, `10226692268`, `11602382893` ($79.99, freezes on the third habit) |
| Feature removed after paying | 21 | 3.4% | 2.19 | `9579733483` ("That is one of the biggest reasons I paid"), `9680451415` |
| Explicit praise of the paid app | 61 simplicity / 58 design | ~9–10% | 2.5–2.7 | even payer praise is now mixed |

These are **segment rates** on a 625-review denominator, not global rates. The equivalent global rates are: entitlement failure 9.02%, upsell to payers 6.05%, support unresponsive 8.87%.

**The one clean counter-example.** `11544732240` (Jul 2024, 5★) bought the $19.99 lifetime *after* reading the 1★ reviews, "wanted to add a review stating I was successful at purchasing and using a lifetime premium upgrade", and — writing as a published app developer — warned that "this series of one star reviews may permanently damage the future of this app". **Interpretation:** the purchase can work. That suggests a code-path or edge-case defect (for example, abandoning the post-purchase "gift" screen, per `10695671832` and `11393421407`), rather than a universally non-functional product.

## 6.6 Trial reaction is net-negative

**56 reviews (1.08%) discuss a trial, mean 2.16, 26 of them 1★.** Causes, per the reviews:
- "free trial" that was actually a paid first year (`6958963175`, `9881090543`, `10542413786` "told I would be getting one year, 365 days free … now only … a month");
- trial renewing into the most expensive plan (`10042115445`);
- trial not visible in iOS subscriptions, so it cannot be cancelled (`9798455172`).

Positive trial outcomes are about **first-year discount** trials, not free trials: `7690833503` ("1 año casi regalado, es más que suficiente para saber si merecen la pena esos 60€" — *"an almost-free year is more than enough to know whether the €60 is worth it"*), `9469085028`.

# PART 7 — COUNTRY AND MARKET ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Eligibility

Storefronts with **≥50 reviews** get individual analysis: **9 storefronts, 4,205 of 5,186 reviews (81.08%)**. The other 71 storefronts (981 reviews, 18.92%) are included in every global number; standalone claims about them are labelled `[limited evidence]`.

## 7.2 All 9 eligible storefronts

Theme rates are % of that storefront's reviews. Non-exclusive. **Rating distributions are language-neutral and should carry the weight; theme rates for non-English storefronts are lower bounds.**

| cc | n | mean | 5★% | 1★% | subst. mean | cap | mon-friction | upsell | price-obj | price-praise | entitlement | billing-integrity | launch | freeze | support-unresp | simple | design | localis. | payer |

| **au** | 193 | 2.37 | 20.7 | 48.2 | 2.32 | **17.1** | **43.5** | **17.6** | 10.9 | 4.1 | **14.0** | **19.7** | 9.3 | 7.8 | 12.4 | 14.0 | 10.4 | 0.0 | 17.6 |
| **de** | 155 | 2.31 | 15.5 | 49.7 | 2.32 | 11.6 | 32.9 | 14.8 | 8.4 | 0.0 | 8.4 | 15.5 | 9.7 | 10.3 | 6.5 | **17.4** | 10.3 | 5.2 | **18.1** |
| **br** | 91 | **1.99** | 14.3 | **64.8** | 2.13 | 9.9 | 24.2 | 2.2 | 11.0 | 5.5 | 9.9 | 13.2 | **28.6** | 4.4 | 5.5 | 13.2 | 9.9 | 5.5 | 14.3 |
| **nl** | 85 | 2.39 | 18.8 | 44.7 | 2.43 | 14.1 | 36.5 | 14.1 | 7.1 | 4.7 | 10.6 | 16.5 | 15.3 | 2.4 | 9.4 | 15.3 | 10.6 | 1.2 | 7.1 |
| **mx** | 57 | 2.11 | 15.8 | 63.2 | 2.00 | 5.3 | 22.8 | 3.5 | 12.3 | 0.0 | 3.5 | 15.8 | **28.1** | 0.0 | 0.0 | 8.8 | 3.5 | 5.3 | 3.5 |
| **fr** | 53 | **2.66** | **28.3** | 45.3 | 2.62 | 9.4 | 20.8 | 9.4 | 3.8 | 0.0 | 3.8 | 15.1 | 11.3 | 9.4 | 9.4 | 15.1 | 5.7 | **11.3** | 9.4 |
| *Global* | 5,186 | 2.36 | 18.8 | 49.6 | 2.34 | 12.9 | 32.0 | 13.0 | 7.3 | 2.3 | 9.0 | 14.8 | 20.3 | 7.8 | 8.9 | 14.0 | 8.8 | 1.2 | 12.1 |

**Era mix matters for comparing countries.** Brazil (41 of 91) and Mexico (26 of 57) are dominated by the 2022 crash wave, while Australia (76 of 193) and Germany (59 of 155) lean toward E4.

## 7.3 United States — n = 2,657 (51.2%), mean 2.34

The US *is* the global picture: every global rate is within ±2 points. It holds most of the E1 enthusiasts (459 E1 reviews) and most E4 integrity complaints (232 of the 468 entitlement failures). US-specific texture:
- the anti-subscription essays (`3441366071`, `8995559986`, `9942976990`);
- teen affordability in 2022–23 (`8024387770`, `8031917452`);
- the clearest diagnoses of the purchase loop (`10695671832`, `11393421407`, `11544732240`).

## 7.4 Canada — n = 404, mean 2.10 — the worst-rated large storefront

- 57.7% 1★.
- Highest price objection among eligible storefronts (11.6%) and a high entitlement-failure rate (13.1%).
- Canadian prices are quoted at CA$12 (2020), CA$53/yr, CA$73.99/yr, CA$24.99 lifetime and a CA$499.99 anchor: `5906389618`, `6841721045`, `9441999855`, `10590258881`, `11689173519`.
- Several of the sharpest legacy grievances are Canadian (`9807076647`, `9833848435`, `9863669849`, `10333369092`, `11613334925`).

## 7.5 Great Britain — n = 510, mean 2.25

- Near-global profile, with a higher upsell rate (16.9%) and freeze rate (11.2%).
- The launch wave hit hard: 118 wave reviews, 22.0% launch failure.
- Price quotes: £51.99/yr, £5.99 → £51.99 intro, £19.99 lifetime, £399 anchor (`9450702497`, `9714201365`, `10323628736`).
- Detailed long-tenure grievances: `10118088583` ("Do not pay for this app (long term review)"), `10430541861`, `10195715445`.

## 7.6 Australia — n = 193, mean 2.37

- **Highest monetisation friction (43.5%), cap (17.1%), upsell (17.6%), entitlement (14.0%) and billing integrity (19.7%)** among eligible storefronts, but *low* launch failure (9.3%).
- A$ quotes: A$10.99 (2019), A$65/yr (2021), A$86.99 (2022), A$129.99/A$29.99 lifetime (2023) — `3749138405`, `6990650128`, `9175246401`, `9390611547`, `10551272227`, `10516950750`.
- **Interpretation:** Australian reviewers experienced the monetisation and integrity story without the crash wave; they are the cleanest view of E4.

## 7.7 Germany — n = 155, mean 2.31

- **Highest simplicity praise (17.4%) and highest payer share (18.1%) among eligible storefronts; zero price praise; 5.2% localisation.** German reviewers pay, praise the design, and object to the model rather than the price level.
- Subscription traps: "Achtung Abo-Falle – kostet automatisch über 100 Euro ab 2. Jahr" (*"Warning, subscription trap – automatically costs over €100 from year 2"*) `7780326363`; "Das Abo läuft trotz Kündigung … weiter" (*"The subscription keeps running despite cancellation"*) `9400136347`.
- Broken lifetime: `10122076030`, `10337807132`.
- The only explicit accessibility review in German: "Keine Bedienungshilfen, kein Darkmode und keine Lokalisierung … mit Sehbehinderung unbrauchbar" (*"No accessibility features, no dark mode, no localisation … unusable with a visual impairment"*) `9043645768`.
- German localisation requests: `5291001643`, `5426529527`, `6845926791`.

## 7.8 Brazil — n = 91, mean 1.99, and Mexico — n = 57, mean 2.11

- **Wave-dominated.** Launch failure is 28.6% (br) and 28.1% (mx); 1★ is 64.8% and 63.2%.
- Upsell complaints are low (2.2%, 3.5%) — **interpretation:** many Latin-American reviewers never got far enough into the app to see the upsell.
- Localisation: 5.5% and 5.3%, in Portuguese and Spanish: `6044639172`, `8230719589`, `5120845317`, `5493530620`.
- Brazil records an unexplained R$299,90 charge (`9648864050`); Mexico quotes 399 pesos/yr and 700 → 2,400 pesos (`6734905163`, `8197069609`).
- **Limited within-country depth: under 100 reviews each.**

## 7.9 Netherlands — n = 85, mean 2.39, and France — n = 53, mean 2.66

- **The Netherlands** has the lowest freeze rate (2.4%), otherwise it is global. Evidence: €10.49 offer charged €59.99 (`8751206704`); the December 2020 "Self-care Christmas" breakage (`6758584064`); "New year, new me" purchase not applied (`10910382150`).
- **France is the best-rated eligible storefront** (mean 2.66, 28.3% 5★) and has the highest localisation demand (11.3%): "J'achèterai la version premium quand elle sera traduite en français" (*"I'll buy the premium version when it's translated into French"*, `2699035298`), `5480365273`, `8312516574`.
- French integrity complaints: "affiché à -50% et finalement je suis débitée la totalité" (*"shown at 50% off and in the end I was charged the full amount"*, `9659959095`); "J'avais annulé mon abonnement et ils ont quand même prélevé" (*"I had cancelled my subscription and they still took payment"*, `10366403685`); a 2020 buyer now asked to re-subscribe (`9845277689`).
- Both are small samples.

## 7.10 Group A — high-spend markets (defined, with disclosure)

**Definition:** `us, cn, jp, gb, de, fr, ca, au, kr` — the storefronts commonly cited as the largest App Store consumer-spend markets. **This is an external-knowledge definition, not derived from this corpus, and no spend or download figure appears anywhere in this data.**

| Group | n | mean | 5★% | 1★% | cap | mon-friction | upsell | support-unresp | launch | localisation | subscription mention | one-time praise |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| High-spend | 4,045 (78.0%) | 2.34 | 18.3 | 49.4 | **14.4** | **34.2** | **14.1** | **9.9** | 19.3 | 0.8 | **18.2** | 0.64 |
| Rest of world (71 storefronts) | 1,141 (22.0%) | 2.42 | 20.6 | 50.1 | 7.5 | 24.1 | 9.4 | 5.3 | **23.8** | **2.6** | 10.9 | 0.09 |

**Findings:**
1. The two groups are **almost identical on overall sentiment** (2.34 vs 2.42; 49.4% vs 50.1% 1★).
2. **What differs is *which* problem people report.** High-spend storefronts report the money story — 1.9× the cap rate, 1.4× monetisation friction, 1.9× support-unresponsive and 1.7× subscription discussion. The rest of the world reports the crash wave (1.2× launch failure) and language (3.3× localisation).
3. **Interpretation:** the monetisation and integrity problems are experienced where people actually attempt to pay. Rest-of-world users more often never got past launch or the English UI. Part of the gap is also classifier coverage (warning 6).

## 7.11 Group B — high-review-volume markets (disclosed proxy)

**Definition:** the 9 storefronts with ≥50 reviews in *this corpus* — `us, gb, ca, au, de, br, nl, mx, fr`. **Review volume is used only as a disclosed proxy for market presence. It is not a download or install figure.**

- **Group B:** n = 4,205 (81.1%), mean **2.30**, 5★ 17.8%, 1★ 50.4%. Cap 14.5% · monetisation friction 34.3% · entitlement 9.3% · billing integrity 15.6% · launch 19.8% · support 9.8%.
- **Tail (71 storefronts):** n = 981, mean **2.58**, 5★ 23.2%, 1★ 46.0%. Cap 6.3% · monetisation friction 21.9% · launch 22.5% · localisation 3.5%.

**Finding:** the tail is *better* rated than the core markets. That is consistent with Group A: the more reviewers engaged with paying, the worse the rating.

## 7.12 Sub-50 storefronts `[limited evidence]`

71 storefronts, 981 reviews (18.92%), all included in every global figure. These observations rise above noise and are flagged:

- **China (n=46, mean 4.09, 52.2% 5★, 8.7% 1★) — the happiest storefront, and the loudest localisation demand (23.9%).** Nearly all reviews are from E1 and ask for Chinese, iCloud sync, a widget or an iPad version, while praising the design: `1384108276` ("已购买 挺好" — *"bought it, it's good"*), `2624985689` ("不是月付终身也才30rmb" — *"not monthly; lifetime was only 30 RMB"*), `4978969793`, `5536840389`, `6218827204` ("无中文无满分" — *"no Chinese, no full marks"*). Later Chinese reviews turn: `7104604747` ("变成订阅制了？？？认真的吗？" — *"it's become a subscription??? seriously?"*), `12066172506` (paid twice, then no membership), `13439321031` ("欺诈" — *"fraud"*).
- **Peru (n=21, mean 1.10, 95.2% 1★), Belgium (n=30, mean 1.73, 70% 1★), South Africa (n=29, 1.90, 72% 1★), Ireland (n=28, 1.96), Colombia (n=27, 2.07), Argentina (n=17, 1.71)** are almost entirely wave storefronts: 71.4% (pe), 46.7% (be), 48.3% (za), 42.9% (ie) and 44.4% (co) launch failure. Examples: `8197216530`, `8203669454`, `8209323125` (pe/co/cl, "no abre" — *"it won't open"*); `8196882583`, `8201773685` (be); `8206024681`, `8198093213` (za).
- **Thailand (24, mean 3.92), Taiwan (18, 3.89), Vietnam (26, 3.38), Malaysia (19, 3.37), Russia (22, 3.36)** are the other positive small storefronts, mostly E1. Malaysia shows 21% platform requests (iPad): `4800486859`, `6155801156`. Russia and Ukraine show 13.6% / 26.7% localisation: `3436084809`, `8252686692`, `8204732391`, `8213466335`.
- **Switzerland (n=23, mean 1.91)** has the highest payer share (30.4%) and upsell rate (30.4%) of any storefront with n ≥ 15: `6948112757`, `8739679081` (a 2018 buyer), `9197717753`, `9855064906` (offered 10 CHF, charged 59 CHF).
- **Spain (n=35)** has the highest cap rate anywhere (28.6%): `6570581596`, `8254179797` ("Una estafa: solo puedes añadir 3 hábitos sin pagar" — *"A scam: you can only add 3 habits without paying"*).
- **Hong Kong (n=15) and India (n=41)** show 26.7% and 17.1% entitlement failure: `2465703291`, `11280869850`, `11763460705` ("I had paid for pro version way back in 2018 … It's fraud if you ask us to pay again"), `10335650591`.

## 7.13 What genuinely varies by country

| Pattern | Where | Section |
|---|---|---|
| Crash-wave share | pe 71%, br 29%, mx 28%, za 48%, be 47%, co 44% vs au 9%, de 10% | 4.1, 7.8, 7.12 |
| Cap complaint | es 29%, au 17%, ca 16%, us 15% vs mx 5%, cn 0% | 7.2 |
| Price objection | ca 11.6%, mx 12.3%, br 11.0%, se 18.2% vs fr 3.8% | 7.2 |
| Entitlement failure | hk 27%, in 17%, au 14%, ca 13% vs mx / fr 3.5–3.8% | 4.3 |
| Payer share | ch 30%, de 18%, au 18% vs mx 3.5% | 6, 7.7 |
| Localisation demand | cn 24%, ua 27%, ru 14%, kr 13%, fr 11%, co 11% | 3.5 |
| Positive small storefronts | cn, th, tw, vn, my, ru — mostly E1 | 7.12 |

No cultural generalisation is drawn beyond these measured differences.

# PART 8 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 8.1 Method

Five eras are defined from product events visible in the reviews (Part 1.6). The rates below are % of each era's reviews. Because E3 contains the 25-day crash wave, E3 is also given *excluding* the wave (n = 731) where it matters. No trend is claimed from monthly data alone.

## 8.2 Trend 1 — Sentiment collapsed in two steps, and has not recovered `[high-priority]`

| Series | E1 | E2 | E3 | E3 excl. wave | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Mean (all) | **3.94** | 2.75 | 1.72 | 2.15 | 2.03 | 2.42 |
| Mean (substantive) | 3.92 | 2.71 | 1.79 | — | 1.93 | 2.27 |
| 1★ share | 11.4% | 32.5% | 68.3% | 51.0% | 56.5% | 46.8% |
| 5★ share | 53.6% | 21.8% | 6.2% | — | 12.2% | 18.5% |

- **Step 1 (E1 → E2)** coincides with the subscription switch and the ownership change: −1.19.
- **Step 2 (E2 → E3/E4)** coincides with the crash wave, then the integrity failures: −0.7 excluding the wave.

E5's partial recovery (2.42) is on a small base (173) and mostly reflects fewer reviews overall, not a new positive theme.

| Free cap | 13.5% | 18.4% | 10.5% | 14.0% | 8.1% |
| Price objection | 4.1% | **13.0%** | 6.1% | 8.1% | 11.6% |

E3's dip is a dilution effect: the wave's 1,135 reviews are about launch, not money.
