- The E1 baseline — 7 entitlement reports in 4.3 years — is the comparator. This is not a constant background rate.
- The E4 step aligns with the dated legacy revocation (Apr 2023) and the dated offer launch (Aug 2023).

## 8.6 Trend 5 — Support went from a strength to a vacuum `[high-priority]`

- Support unresponsive: **0.6% (E1) → 5.1% → 3.1% → 20.3% (E4) → 20.8% (E5)**. By year: 1 · 0 · 0 · 3 · 12 · 20 · 56 · **191 · 141** · 29 · 7.
- Support praised (18 reviews): **11 in E1**, 2 in E2, 4 in E4, 1 in E5.

## 8.7 Trend 6 — Reliability: one spike, then a slow structural decline `[high-priority]`

| Theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Launch failure | 1.2% | 1.8% | **53.6%** | 1.6% | 2.9% |
| Freeze / lag | 3.1% | 5.4% | 8.0% | 9.0% | **26.6%** |
| Counts wrong | 1.0% | 1.4% | 1.8% | 3.1% | 2.9% |
| Health data wrong | 0.4% | 0.0% | 0.3% | 1.5% | 2.3% |
| Data loss | 1.3% | 2.6% | 1.4% | 2.1% | 3.5% |

- The launch spike was fixed and did not recur at scale.
- **Lag is the persistent structural problem, and its share rises in every era.** By 2025–26, one review in four is about lag.

## 8.8 Trend 7 — Redesign regressions cluster in 2023 `[very strong in E4]`

- Feature removal: 0.6% → 1.1% → 0.9% → **9.4%** → 5.8%.
- An update blamed for a regression: 0.9% → 1.1% → 3.0% → **8.4%** → 4.1%.
- Streak removal (52) and swipe removal (41) are **100% E4**.
- Monthly/yearly: 3 (late 2022) · 41 (2023) · 7 (2024) · 5 (2025) · 2 (2026). The request is still arriving three years after the removal.

## 8.9 Trend 8 — Praise thinned, and switching reversed direction `[very strong]`

| Praise theme | E1 | E2 | E3 | E4 | E5 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Simplicity | 34.2% | 20.2% | 5.5% | 10.0% | 12.7% |
| Design | 19.3% | 9.7% | 4.1% | 7.9% | 6.9% |
| Motivation / streaks | 11.8% | 6.1% | 2.4% | 5.3% | 3.5% |
| Life outcome | 6.0% | 4.4% | 1.3% | 2.4% | 3.5% |
| Customisation | 7.9% | 4.4% | 1.1% | 3.6% | 7.5% |
| Competitor named | 9.5% (arrivals) | 4.7% | 2.0% | 5.1% (departures) | 2.9% |

## 8.10 Trend 9 — Demand shifted from platforms to fixing the basics `[meaningful]`

- Platform requests (iPad / Mac / landscape) fall from **5.3% of E1 to about 1% after 2021**.
- Widget mentions spike to **14.0% of E2** — the iOS 14 home-screen widget gap, 73 of 451 reviews between Sep 2020 and Sep 2021.
- Localisation falls from 2.8% (E1) to 0.4% (E4).

**Interpretation:** the users who asked for expansion left or stopped asking; later reviewers ask for the app to work, honour purchases and stop interrupting.

## 8.11 Trends explicitly NOT claimed

- **No review-prompt trend.** 20 complaints (0.39%), 9 in E1; short reviews ≤7.7% in every era. The rating is not prompt-inflated.
- **No ADHD trend.** 33 reviews over ten years (4 · 3 · 8 · 14 · 4).
- **No Watch trend.** 60 problem reviews spread across eras; the 2022 break and repair (`8724502286`) is recorded, not trended.
- **No monthly trend within E4.** Months range from 1.50 to 2.64 on n = 52–280.
- **No data-loss trend.** Flat at 1.3–3.5%.
- **No causal claim linking TikTok to the crash.** They co-occur; the corpus cannot establish that one caused the other.
- **No claim that lag is deliberate "throttling".** Three reviewers infer it (`9836149317`, `9902067482`, `12138194811`); the corpus cannot test it.

---

# PART 9 — WHAT A COMPETITOR LEARNS FROM THIS CORPUS FOR FREE

1. **Honouring past purchases is worth more than any feature.** 189 legacy buyers publicly turned from advocates into accusers (`9833924285` wrote the top "Done app review" article before writing a 1★). A competitor offering "import your Done data, keep your one-time price" is quoting this corpus.
2. **A trustworthy one-time price was this app's original moat.** 27 E1 reviewers (mean 4.78) chose Done *because* it wasn't a subscription, and 13 later reviews leave for Streaks because it still is one-time (`13036517547`).
3. **Fake urgency backfires in a self-improvement category.** 111 countdown complaints, a "Groundhog Day" reference, and "the only habit I'm developing is closing the upsell pop up" (`10226738858`).
4. **Data export is the exit door; keep it open and fast.** The CSV hang (`14121729788`) is cited as a lock-in.
5. **Monthly and yearly frequencies are a real niche.** 58 reviews asked for them back, some in 2026; relationships, chores, contact lenses, vacations, dentist visits.
6. **Build + quit in one list, "N times per week", musical sounds, and a colourful minimal UI are the category-winning basics.** 458 simplicity and 250 design praise reviews prove the brief.
7. **A crash-on-launch during an acquisition spike is catastrophic.** 35% of lifetime 1★ in 25 days. Stage rollouts before campaigns.
8. **ADHD and health-tracking users are the most loyal and the most hurt by regressions.** 33 ADHD reviews (mean 4.09); medication and sobriety streak users (`8583843842`, `9560996028`, `9561693468`).

---

# PART 10 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Every recommendation cites the findings it rests on.

## 10.1 Immediate — purchase integrity (top of the list)

1. **Honour every historical purchase automatically.** `[4.3a, 6.5, 8.5]` Validate receipts for the pre-2020 one-time Pro, the 2020–21 subscriptions and the bundle, and grandfather them into today's Premium without user action. 189 legacy failures (mean 1.38), 125 of them explicit payers, and most describe support refusing or ignoring them.
2. **Fix the offer → purchase → entitlement path end to end.** `[4.3a, 6.5]` 295 reviews (mean 1.14) paid for "lifetime" and stayed on Basic. The corpus points at the post-purchase "gift" / Growth-Bundle screen: abandoning it appears to drop the entitlement (`10695671832`, `11393421407`, `10639028732`). One clean success (`11544732240`) shows it can work. Make the entitlement grant atomic with the purchase, then show any cross-sell *after*.
3. **Make the displayed price the charged price.** `[4.3b]` 69 reviews; checkout showed $59.99–$99.99 against a $8.99–$15.99 banner. Audit the offer-code / introductory-offer configuration per storefront, starting with CA, AU, GB and DE.
4. **Put "Manage subscription" in the app and show the renewal price before purchase.** `[4.2d, 4.3c]` 66 cancellation traps, 50 renewal shocks.
5. **Staff support with a working, single contact path and a published response time.** `[Exec 2, 8.6]` 460 unresponsive reviews. Fix the dead typeform links and the Mail-app-only link (`7324774905`, `8381379162`, `10451006156`). Resolve by receipt, not by argument.

## 10.2 Immediate — stop the upsell from breaking the product

6. **Never show an upsell to anyone with any active or historical entitlement.** `[4.2b, 6.5]` 314 reviews, mean 1.57.
7. **Remove fake countdowns and the double pop-up; cap offers at one dismissible surface per week.** `[4.2b]` 111 + 19 reviews. **Interpretation:** a resetting "limited-time" timer is a dark pattern that several jurisdictions' consumer-protection regimes scrutinise; that is an external consideration, not a corpus finding.
8. **Never let a promotional element occupy a habit row or slow the list.** `[4.2b, 4.4]` `6751801484` (hid a habit), `9819638240` (30 s per mark).

## 10.3 Immediate — reliability and data trust

9. **Set a tap-to-register latency budget (<100 ms) and test with multi-year histories.** `[4.4, 8.7]` Freeze/lag is 26.6% of 2025–26 reviews; `12066404983` says it degrades with entry count.
10. **Store completions against the local calendar day, not a UTC timestamp.** `[4.4]` 13 time-zone reviews, including 600- and 1,500-day streaks.
11. **Respect the week-start setting everywhere, including widgets and the list view.** `[4.4]` 30 reviews.
12. **De-duplicate Apple Health imports; show the source value alongside Done's value.** `[4.4]` 37 reviews of doubled steps and sleep.
13. **Gate releases on data-migration tests and stage every rollout.** `[4.1, 4.4]` 1.19.0 (launch crash), May 2022 (hang), 2.7.1/2.7.2 (count corruption) were all shipped to everyone.
14. **Make backup, restore, sync and export actually work, and keep export fast.** `[4.4]` 83 sync/backup problems; export hangs.

## 10.4 Near-term product — restore what was removed

15. **Bring back monthly and yearly frequency.** `[4.5]` 58 reviews, still arriving in 2026.
16. **Show the streak on the main list (optional toggle) and restore swipe-between-days alongside the calendar.** `[4.5]` 52 + 41 reviews.
17. **Restore quit-to-zero goals, custom ordering, "last done", Shortcuts and an interactive widget with chosen habits.** `[4.5]` `8479953821`, `9689878548`, `9540594680`, `8463235133`, `9050514368`.
18. **Stop moving an edited habit to the top of the list.** `[4.5]` `11518468921`, `13467130073`, `9338298616`.

## 10.5 Monetisation experiments (after 10.1–10.2 are fixed)

19. **Offer a monthly plan and a genuine one-time lifetime price.** `[3.3, 6.3, 7.10]` 8 monthly-plan requests; 27 E1 one-time praises; 106 one-time-removed objections. Test lifetime vs annual vs monthly on conversion *and* 1★ rate.
20. **Test the cap: 3 vs 5–6 habits vs a 14-day full trial.** `[3.3, 4.2a]` The evaluation argument ("I can't tell if it works with 3") is the plurality objection since 2016.
21. **Lead with a student / low-income price in markets with the 2022–23 TikTok cohort.** `[4.2e]` 110 teen and low-income reviews.

## 10.6 Platform and access

22. **Native iPad layout with landscape; real-time iCloud sync across iPhone, iPad and Watch.** `[3.5]` 98 platform + 83 sync reviews, concentrated in the 4★ band.
23. **VoiceOver labels, Dynamic Type, 44 pt targets.** `[3.5]` `6988004094`, `8200488157`, `9043645768`, `8234373819`.
24. **Localise into Spanish, Portuguese, Chinese, German, French and Russian.** `[3.5, 7.12]` 61 requests; French and German reviewers say localisation would trigger purchase (`2699035298`, `5426529527`).

## 10.7 Verification questions this report cannot answer, ranked

25. **How many legacy one-time/lifetime purchasers exist, and what share are currently shown as Basic?** `[4.3a]` Checkable in the receipt database; determines the size of the goodwill fix.
26. **Does abandoning the post-purchase "gift" screen drop the entitlement?** `[4.3a, 6.5]` Reproduce `10695671832`.
27. **Which storefronts' introductory offers resolve to the full price at checkout?** `[4.3b]`
28. **What is the tap latency distribution by device and by history length?** `[4.4]`
29. **Was the Dec 2021 crash specific to 1.19.0 on first launch (fresh install) vs update?** `[4.1]` Wave reviews include both new installers and 2015-era users.
30. **Are any renewals billed outside App Store subscriptions?** `[4.3c]` Two reviewers claim so (`10323742685`, `11443832766`); almost certainly a misunderstanding, but a statement of fact from the developer would retire it.

## 10.8 Experiments worth running

31. **Upsell frequency: every launch vs weekly vs never-for-past-payers**, measuring conversion, D30 retention and 1★ rate. `[10.2]`
32. **Lifetime vs annual vs monthly price architecture.** `[10.5]`
33. **Cap 3 vs 6 vs trial.** `[10.5]`
34. **Streak-on-list toggle default on vs off**, measuring logging frequency. `[4.5]`

# PART 11 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 11.1 Counting rules

- The denominator for every global percentage is **5,186** (all records, no removals). The substantive series, where stated, is **4,267** (body > 60 characters).
- Era denominators: E1 = 941, E2 = 570, E3 = 1,866 (731 excluding the 22 Dec 2021 – 15 Jan 2022 wave), E4 = 1,636, E5 = 173.
- Country denominators: that storefront's own record count.
- Segment denominators, always labelled as such: explicit payers 625; 5★ 976; 4★ 452; 3★ 581; 2★ 606; 1★ 2,571; E4 1★ 924; E3 1★ 1,274.
- Themes are **non-exclusive**. Unions (`MON_FRICTION`, `BILLING_INTEGRITY`, `RELIABILITY`, `FEATURE_REMOVAL`) are computed on distinct review IDs.
- Sub-flags (`ent_legacy`, `ent_new_offer`, `promo_charged`) are subsets of their parent theme; a small number of entitlement reviews carry both legacy and new-offer flags.
- Percentages are rounded to 2 dp globally and 1 dp in country and era tables. Means are rounded to 2 dp.
- Manual qualitative splits (Part 3.3) are labelled as such and are **not** additional quantified themes.

## 11.2 Theme definitions

| Theme key | Counts a review when it contains |
|---|---|
| `free_cap` | a numeral 2–3 (or word form, 8 languages) within 28 chars of a habit/goal/task/item word, **and** a money/limit word within ±140 chars; or "only/more than 2–3 …"; or "free version is limited" (4 only with tight pay context, widget excluded) |
| `paywall_general` | "have to pay", "paywall", "not free", "useless unless", "pay for everything", "de pago", "payant", "ücretli", etc. |
| `price_objection` | expensive / overpriced / greedy / rip-off / money grab / can't afford (strong), or ridiculous / absurd / insane / steep / not worth within 70 chars of a price word (weak) |
| `price_praise` | worth it / affordable / fair price / good value / "vale la pena" / "lohnt sich", with negated "not worth" removed first |
| `one_time_mention` / `one_time_praise` | one-time / one-off / pay once / lifetime / no subscription; praise = that ∧ 4–5★ ∧ before 31 Aug 2020 |
| `one_time_removed` | used-to-be-one-time → now subscription; removed / bring back one-time; "pricing has changed" |
| `upsell_nag` | pop-up, banner, splash, full-screen, countdown, "claim my offer", "gift to yourself", Growth Bundle, upsell, pester, harass, "ads for / to …", "keep asking me to upgrade" (+ 9 languages) |
| `ads_to_payers` | `upsell_nag` ∧ a first-person already-paid / premium-member phrase |
| `fake_countdown` | countdown / timer / "ends in" / "limited time" with reset / again / never / always / months |
| `double_popup` | upsell ∧ ≥ 2024 ∧ twice / two pop-ups / double |
| `bundle_forced` | bundle ∧ only option / forced / don't want / just this app |
| `renewal_jump` | renew / next year / after first year / jumps to ∧ price ∧ ≤3★ |
| `entitlement_failure` | paid earlier ∧ (again / restore / lost / basic / not honoured / subscription …); pay twice; restore fails; lifetime not honoured; paid ∧ still Basic / not unlocked / keeps asking to buy — **then curated (11.3)** |
| `promo_charged_full` | charged full/regular price; "instead of $X"; claim offer → $59.99 shown; offer … charged $59/60/73/86/99 — **then curated** |
| `billing_dispute` | refund, charged, unauthorised, scam, fraud, stole, dispute, chargeback + 12 languages |
| `cancel_difficulty` | can't / how to / no option to cancel or unsubscribe; charged after cancelling — **plus curated additions** |
| `launch_fail` | won't / can't / doesn't open, load, launch, let me in; crash / close / kick-out on open / tap; back to the home screen; stuck on intro / loading; + 20 languages; **plus a wave rule** (22 Dec 2021 – 28 Feb 2022, ≤3★, crash / glitch / kick / won't work vocabulary, excluding pure cap complaints) |
| `freeze_lag` | freeze, lag, slow, sluggish, unresponsive, hang, delay, "takes N seconds", + 10 languages |
| `data_loss` | lost / deleted / wiped / reset / corrupted ∧ data / habits / streaks / history |
| `count_wrong` | wrong / incorrect / doesn't match / doubles / randomly adds / extra count / 5-digit counts |
| `health_wrong` | Health / steps / sleep within 140 chars of wrong / double / off / not syncing / glitch |
| `watch_broken` | Watch / complication within 160 chars of not working / crash / blank / out of sync / useless |
| `sync_problem` | sync / iCloud / Dropbox / backup / restore within 80 chars of a failure or need word |
| `support_unresponsive` | no / never / haven't … response / reply / heard back; support … nonexistent / ignored / useless; "won't respond"; broken contact / typeform / email bounce — **plus curated additions** |
| `support_positive` | **hand-read list** (18 IDs) |
| `update_regression` | update / new version / redesign ∧ worse / broke / removed / bring back / crash / lag; or "since the update …" |
| `monthly_yearly_request` | **hand-read list** (58 IDs, all ≥ Dec 2022) |
| `streak_removed` | ≥ 15 Jan 2023 ∧ streak ∧ main page / front page / removed / bring back / can't see — **plus curated additions** |
| `swipe_removed` | ≥ 15 Jan 2023 ∧ "swip…" |
| `adhd` | ADHD, TDAH, ADHS, СДВГ, autism, Asperger, OCD, Tourette, neurodivergent, executive function, explicit "I have ADD" |
| `payer` | first-person paid / bought / purchased / upgraded / subscribed, "premium member", "paying customer", + 12 languages, excluding conditional phrasings |
| Others | See `analyze.py` in 11.5 for the exact patterns of all 86 theme keys |

## 11.3 Manual corrections applied (full lists)

The full ID lists for every theme after correction are in 11.6. The corrections themselves:

- **`entitlement_failure`:** removed 97 regex false positives (ads-after-paying only, crash-only, cap complaints, subscription-model objections without a lost purchase, promo-price issues, cancellation issues). Added 136 real cases found in the recall read. Final **468**.
- **`promo_charged_full`:** removed 33 (e.g. "instead of 0", "3 goals instead of 5", renewal shocks, unauthorised charges, cancellation charges). Added 16 (`8523180720`, `8527835943`, `9343230044`, `9837012271`, `9863827506`, `10855037686`, `9664317736`, `8797086033`, `9320694352`, `9450067550`, `9634611645`, `9693239507`, `9975503064`, `10097186671`, `11418686273`, `8896192046`). Final **69**.
- **`streak_removed`:** added 24 (`9559035583`, `9559832988`, `9560644479`, `9560988014`, `9562255131`, `9566841290`, `9569526535`, `9570927355`, `9571529511`, `9585668347`, `9589974405`, `9592185239`, `9593284243`, `9597726823`, `9601489372`, `9603533792`, `9622188814`, `9634076132`, `9548573400`, `10266641762`, `9677629681`, `9560780539`, `9561580894`, `10497376233`). Final **52**.
- **`cancel_difficulty`:** added 22 from the recall read (`8513573317`, `9360518657`, `9400136347`, `9509987159`, `9582319910`, `9614387990`, `9634334594`, `9798455172`, `10212296472`, `10323742685`, `10353424621`, `10366403685`, `10771826228`, `10834296339`, `11217668995`, `11594644730`, `12131407578`, `13262318239`, `9623050748`, `12263878766`, `9551358254`, `9631325382`). Final **66**.
- **`launch_fail`:** removed `1614172377`, `3880826227`, `9896369010`, `11758825782`, `12004489241`, `12203287883`, `12253790678`, `8194034455`; added wave-phrasing patterns and the wave rule. Final **1,052**.
- **`support_unresponsive`:** added `8591341012`, `9570932737`, `10844244287`, `10931712000`, `11297575909`, `9551358254` plus "won't / don't respond" phrasings (≤3★); removed `9585668347`, `12073546027`. Final **460**.
- **`monthly_yearly_request`:** replaced by a hand-read list (the regex matched "$60 a year … option"). Final **58**.
- **`adhd`:** dropped the ambiguous bare-"add" rule. Final **33**.
- **`payer`:** conditional phrasings excluded; `6955037783`, `8890581679`, `9441689525`, `8071903682`, `7225270093` removed by hand. Final **625**.
- **`support_positive`:** hand-read list; `3332657537` and `4952817490` removed on re-read (they are about upsell restraint and a purchase, not support). Final **18**.

## 11.4 Validation approach and known error risk

- **Control:** all 5,186 records were read in full, in date order, before any classifier was written. Reading notes with review IDs were kept for every batch.
- **Precision checks:** every match was read for `streak_removed`, `promo_charged_full`, `adhd`, `entitlement_failure` (all 455 regex matches) and `monthly_yearly_request`. Targeted false-positive scans were run on `support_unresponsive` (lag / "unresponsive" confusion), `launch_fail` (all 4–5★ and link / export contexts), `free_cap` (all 4–5★) and `payer` (conditional phrasings).
- **Recall checks:** broad-pattern sweeps of untagged reviews for 8 themes (streak, promo, monthly, entitlement, ADHD, launch, cancel, support). All candidates in the reviewed subsets were read, and additions are listed in 11.3.
- **Direction of residual error:** under-counting in non-English text (especially ja, ko, zh, th, vi, ar, tr, id). Over-counting risk remains highest in `billing_dispute` (the word "charged" appears in neutral contexts), `simple` / `design_praise` (used in complaints — hence the 4–5★ subsets), and `subscription_mention` (a mention theme, not a complaint theme).
- **Not validated by a second rater.** A single analyst read the corpus and wrote the patterns.

## 11.5 External sources and reproduction

| Claim | Source | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Membership of "high-spend markets" (Part 7.10) | External general knowledge of the largest App Store consumer-spend storefronts | **Not derived from this corpus. No spend or download figure appears in this data.** Disclosed as an assumption. |
| "High-review-volume markets" (Part 7.11) | This corpus's own review counts | Disclosed proxy. **Not downloads.** |
| Developer "Kodeon, Inc.", bundle `com.tbd.Done` | `manifest.json` | Corpus metadata |
| Everything else | The 5,186 reviews | Direct |

**No product website, App Store listing, press material or third-party data was consulted.** Where reviewers contradict each other (the free cap being 2, 3, 4 or 5; the price in a given year), the contradiction is reported rather than resolved.

```
cd "App Store Reviews/31. Do Habits - Get It Done - Daily Routine & Goal Planner"

# 1. reconcile: records == unique ids == sum(by_country) == manifest.total == 5,186;
#    per-country and rating distributions == manifest
# 2. emit readable batches (~55 KB each, date order, 26 batches), read all 26 in full
#    format: review_id|country|rating|date|title :: body   (double HTML-unescaped, whitespace collapsed;
#    lines > 1,500 chars wrapped, never truncated)
# 3. analyze.py  — 76 multilingual regex themes over lowercased title+body, + 4 unions
# 4. overrides.py — tightened rules + the curated include/exclude ID lists in 11.3
# 5. stats.py    — aggregates by rating / country / year / month / era, with and without
#                  the 22 Dec 2021 – 15 Jan 2022 wave
# 6. spot-read every match of each high-impact theme; recall-sweep untagged reviews for 8 themes
```
